The brief

Your full brief is in (manager scratchpad)/elem_people.txt. Read it completely first and work from it. You're a study-artist learning to draw the people of pre-contact Washington at small scale, with dignity and accuracy; your folder is ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/.

The human-ecology naturalist has just finished guides written partly for you, in ~/work/naturalist/people/:

  • 00-start-here.md;
  • 03-dwellings-and-villages.md (with painter guidance by distance);
  • 06-dress-tools-crafts-canoes.md (clothing by region, season and class, tools, canoes, and notes on drawing figures at small pixel scale);
  • photos/INDEX.md (photo folders, already pruned of inappropriate images). It recommends targeting about AD 1400–1730: before horses and before smallpox. Rely on it for accuracy, and depict with care and respect for living peoples: no ceremonial or sacred subjects. You own your approach.

You're a student learning to paint one subject beautifully, in the painterly pixel-art language of Mark Ferrari (with Studio Ghibli background painting as a kindred spirit): **people of pre-contact Washington: individuals and groups at work, at rest and at play**.

The people in voxsim's world are the heart of its stories, and today they're tiny, barely readable figures. The target is Washington before European contact: people in cedar-bark and hide clothing, woven hats, robes; fishing, weaving, carrying, splitting cedar, gathering camas, tending a fire, children playing, a group around a hearth. Learn to draw them so their activity and weight read instantly, from about 30 px tall down to 6 px in a landscape, with dignity and without caricature.

# Human ecology desk: start here

Field guides on how people lived in Washington before European contact: the outer coast, the Salish Sea, the rainforest valleys, the prairies, the mountains and the Columbia Plateau. Written for voxsim's sim lanes (above all vox-human) and for its painters. Each guide has **Part A for sim builders** (processes, numbers, what to model) and **Part B for painters** (what it looks like at each distance and season, with photo pointers).

## The guides

| # | Guide | Read it for |
|---|---|---|
| 01 | [Peoples, regions and deep time](01-peoples-regions-and-deep-time.md) | who lived where; population and density; a 14,000-year chronology including the earthquakes, slides and lahars people lived through; **which era vox-human should target (late precontact, AD ~1400–1730)** |
| 02 | [Seasonal rounds](02-seasonal-rounds.md) | month by month (with core sim days): winter villages, shellfish camps, camas prairies, berry grounds, weirs; Hood Canal, outer coast, prairies, Plateau |
| 03 | [Dwellings and villages](03-dwellings-and-villages.md) | plank houses (measured: posts, rafters, planks, platforms, racks, mats), mat houses, Plateau pit houses and mat lodges, sweat houses, palisades; where villages went |
| 04 | [Fishing, shellfish and the stored-food economy](04-fishing-and-shellfish.md) | weirs, dip nets, reef nets, Celilo and Kettle Falls; how much fish; drying and pounding; clams and middens |
| 05 | [Plant foods, cedar and fire](05-plants-gathering-and-fire.md) | camas, lomatiums, bitterroot, wapato, berries (harvest rates in kg/day); earth ovens; cedar planks from living trees and bark stripping; fire on prairies and berry fields |
| 06 | [Dress, bodies, tools, crafts and canoes](06-dress-tools-crafts-canoes.md) | what people wore (by region, class, season, and before/after horses), measured bodies, tools and materials, canoes, baskets and blankets; **figure-drawing notes for small-scale pixel art** |

## Part B. For the painters

### B1. The plank house at each distance

- **Close (1–3 m):** Split cedar planks show long, shallow adze scallops, like hammered metal in wood. Fresh-split cedar is **salmon-pink to warm orange-red**, fading within a year or two to **silver-grey** outside, with dark water streaks under the eaves and green moss and lichen on the roof troughs. Inside everything is **smoke-blackened**: rafters glossy black-brown, planks sooty, with shafts of light through the propped-open roof planks and the smoke hanging in a layer at rafter height. Cattail mats on the walls are straw-gold to grey-tan with fine vertical ribbing every few cm (the sewing lines). Red-painted or carved house posts (Puget Sound posts carried the owner's spirit helper, 3–5 ft figures in relief on the inner face; snakes, fish, lizards, sea mammals and human figures, "with nothing of the peculiar style" of the northern coast; Waterman & Greiner). Racks of split salmon hang overhead, red flesh going dark mahogany as it cures.
- **10 m:** A long, low, grey-brown wall of vertical or horizontal planks with visible battens and overlaps. The roof is a very gentle single slope toward the back (Puget Sound) with a ribbed tile pattern of troughed planks, weighted here and there with stones or poles. Smoke seeps from a few propped planks along the roof, not from a chimney. Dogs, drying racks and canoes are drawn up on the beach in front. In autumn the roofs themselves carry fish or berries drying.
- **100 m:** A row of 3–5 big grey boxes side by side, parallel to the beach, above the drift-log line, with smaller houses at the ends. The dark forest wall stands directly behind, the gravel or shell beach and canoes in front. Blue woodsmoke lies flat along the row on still winter mornings. Shell middens form white-grey banks along the beach front and at the house ends, with nettles growing on them.
- **1 km, and from the air:** A thin line of grey dots at a river mouth or on a spit, easily missed, the only straight edges in the landscape. Canoe traffic makes V-wakes toward it. In summer the frames stand bare, an open lattice of posts and rafters with no walls.

### B2. The Plateau at each distance

- **Pit house, close:** a low dome or cone of earth and grass, **the same colour as the ground** (buff and sage in summer, snow-covered in winter). A ladder or notched log sticks up out of the smoke hole with smoke rising around it. Circular depressions of old abandoned pits pock the terrace nearby. These "housepits" are still visible today on Columbia and Snake terraces.
- **Mat lodge, 10 m:** long, low and tawny, like a gigantic loaf of bread with rounded ends. The tule mats have a vertical grain and the tule has a pale **straw-yellow to soft grey-green** sheen, weathering grey. Poles are laid across to hold the mats, and there are one to four smoke holes along the ridge.
- **Fishing camp at a falls, 100 m:** mat lodges and brush shade shelters on bare basalt benches, and red s

# What earlier students discovered: notes on their craft

These are notes on the painstaking work of the students who came before you:

- four who each spent hours learning Ferrari's language on one small scene (`pedagogy/{atelier,master-copy,analysis,observation}`);
- seven who studied single topics.

They copied his pixels crop by crop, measured his grass against real grass, built a boulder up from a lit sphere, and diagrammed his picture before daring to paint it. What they found is here to inspire you and save you time. It isn't a specification. You'll see things they didn't, and some of what they wrote will turn out wrong or incomplete for your subject. Follow your own eyes. Their best work is in their intermediate studies (e.g. `master-copy/out/cmp_c*.png`, `atelier/stage2/`, `observation/studies/translate_*.png`, `analysis/img/`); those are worth looking at more than their final painters.

The tags below say how each finding came about:

- **[shown]**: a student built it and it worked.
- **[measured]**: a student measured it in Ferrari's pixels or in photos.
- **[mine]**: the integrator's reading, not yet tried by anyone.

Striking to us: the four students, working apart, found much the same underlying ideas.

## The convergence

Working alone, all four arms landed on the same architecture:

- **Paint indices, not colours.**
  - Master copy found that Ferrari's index map is literally a form map: indices are bins of surface direction, and "the palette is the light". The same index image serves morning and afternoon. [measured]
  - Atelier paints in "step space" along one material ramp. [shown]
  - Sky found that two of Ferrari's hour pairs are identical pixels. [measured]
  - Analysis found that value is the structure and colour is temperature and distance. [measured]
- **Two light families that never touch.** Atelier's terminator rule [shown], analysis's three-value picture plan with every mass kept inside its group [measured], and rock's lit and shadow groups [shown] are the same idea at two scales.
- **Dither mixes only adjacent steps of one ramp.** All four arms use it. Ordered 2×2 dither suits hard or precise things (rock, sky seams, water rows); random or diffusion dither suits organic texture (lawns, foam). Master copy measured this [measured]; analysis cites Ferrari's GDC talk for the same split.
- **The medium's own geometry.** Contours are clean runs (1:1, 2:1, 4-4-4 flats), measured on Ferrari's crests by analysis and atelier [measured]. Atelier redraws every major contour by snapping edges to clean ratios [shown]. Masses meet level ground or water in a dead-flat run with a 1-px darker contact line [measured: analysis, observation].
- **Clean runs are for narrow forms; mass boundaries are where texture ends.** David's correction (2026-09-25): the clean-run rule came from the *grass strokes*. It applies to anything narrow, drawn as a line: blades, cracks, joints, twigs, conifer spires, stems, a snowline drawn as a stroke. A 1–2 px fo

# output:
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""imgfetch: fast, polite reference-image sourcing (Wikimedia Commons + Openverse).

  python3 imgfetch.py "cinder cone scoria slope" -n 12 -o refs/cinder
  python3 imgfetch.py -f queries.txt -n 8 -o refs            # one query per line; subdir per query
  options: --src commons,openverse   --width 1200   --sheet (writes <out>/_sheet.jpg)

Writes images plus <out>/sources.tsv (file, title, license, author, page url).
Cached: files already present are skipped. Handles 429/5xx with Retry-After backoff.
Parallel downloads (4 workers) with a shared per-host rate limiter (~3 req/s; Wikimedia's media
host is kept to 1 per 2 s and slows further on any 429, since it throttles hard per IP after bursts).
Openverse (mostly Flickr-hosted CC images) is the primary source because it stays fast.
"""
import argparse, concurrent.futures as cf, json, os, re, sys, threading, time, urllib.error, urllib.parse, urllib.request

UA = "pixelart-studies-imgfetch/1.0 (research; contact: local)"
_locks = {}; _last = {}; _glock = threading.Lock()

SLOW_HOSTS = {"upload.wikimedia.org": 2.0}   # Wikimedia media host: ~1 req/s or it 429s

def _throttle(host, min_gap=0.34):
    min_gap = SLOW_HOSTS.get(host, min_gap)
    with _glock:
        lk = _locks.setdefault(host, threading.Lock())
    with lk:
        dt = time.time() - _last.get(host, 0)
        if dt < min_gap:
            time.sleep(min_gap - dt)
        _last[host] = time.time()

def get(url, tries=4, timeout=25):
    host = urllib.parse.urlparse(url).netloc
    if host == "upload.wikimedia.org":
        tries = 2  # fail fast; other sources fill in
    for i in range(tries):
        _throttle(host)
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA})
            return urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout).read()

"""Willow palettes: (material, step) -> RGB for a season, an hour and a depth.

Light as hue shift: every ramp walks from a cool dark (tinted by the sky's ambient colour) to a warm
light (tinted by the sun). Depth lifts and cools the darks and leaves the lights nearly alone
(measured on Ferrari by the master-copy study). Silver is its own ramp: the undersides of willow
leaves, grey-green to pale silver, cooler than the green.
"""
import numpy as np
from wpaint import SKY, GRAVEL, LEAF, SILVER, STEM, WATER, GRASS, EARTH, FAR

LEAFS = 10


def _lin(c):
    c = np.asarray(c, float) / 255
    return np.where(c > 0.04045, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4, c / 12.92)


def _srgb(l):
    l = np.clip(l, 0, 1)
    return np.where(l > 0.0031308, 1.055 * l ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055, 12.92 * l) * 255


def to_lab(c):
    l = _lin(c)
    M1 = np.array([[0.4122214708, 0.5363325363, 0.0514459929], [0.2119034982, 0.6806995451, 0.1073969566],
                   [0.0883024619, 0.2817188376, 0.6299787005]])
    lms = np.cbrt(M1 @ l)
    M2 = np.array([[0.2104542553, 0.7936177850, -0.0040720468], [1.9779984951, -2.4285922050, 0.4505937099],
                   [0.0259040371, 0.7827717662, -0.8086757660]])
    return M2 @ lms


def to_rgb(lab):
    M2i = np.array([[1, 0.3963377774, 0.2158037573], [1, -0.1055613458, -0.0638541728], [1, -0.0894841775, -1.2914855480]])
    lms = (M2i @ lab) ** 3
    M1i = np.array([[4.0767416621, -3.3077115913, 0.2309699292], [-1.2684380046, 2.6097574011, -0.3413193965],
                    [-0.0041960863, -0.7034186147, 1.7076147010]])
    return tuple(int(round(v)) for v in _srgb(M1i @ lms))


def mix(a, b, t):
    return to_rgb(to_lab(a) * (1 - t) + to_lab(b) * t)


def ramp(dark, light, n, sun, amb, warm=0.25, cool=0.3):
    """n steps from dark to light in OKLab; lights pulled toward the sun colour, darks toward ambient."""
    out = []
    for i in range(n):
        t = i / max(n - 1, 1)
        c = to_lab(dark) * (1 - t) + to_lab(light) * t
        c = c * (1 - warm * t ** 1.5) + to_lab(sun) * warm * t ** 1.5 if warm else c
        c = c * (1 - cool * (1 - t) ** 2) + to_lab(amb) * cool * (1 - t) ** 2 if cool else c
        out.append(to_rgb(c))
    return out


SEASONS = {
    #          lit base (dark, light)          lit leaves (mid, top)          silver (lo, hi)            twig (dark, light)       shadow (dark, mark)
    "summer": dict(base=((14, 36, 18), (30, 62, 30)), leaf=((92, 132, 56), (168, 200, 96)), silver=((120, 142, 128), (196, 214, 200)),
                   twig=((50, 36, 30), (150, 120, 84)), shadow=((6, 24, 14), (36, 66, 42))),

    "silvery": dict(base=((16, 32, 26), (36, 58, 46)), leaf=((104, 128, 96), (170, 190, 150)), silver=((136, 156, 150), (216, 228, 222)),
                    twig=((54, 40,
Write: elements/people/src/util.py
"""Small helpers: OKLab, upscaling, labelled grids, side-by-side panels."""
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont


def _lin(c):
    c = np.asarray(c, float) / 255
    return np.where(c > 0.04045, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4, c / 12.92)


def _srgb(l):
    l = np.clip(l, 0, 1)
    return np.where(l > 0.0031308, 1.055 * l ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055, 12.92 * l) * 255


_M1 = np.array([[0.4122214708, 0.5363325363, 0.0514459929], [0.2119034982, 0.6806995451, 0.1073969566],
                [0.0883024619, 0.2817188376, 0.6299787005]])
_M2 = np.array([[0.2104542553, 0.7936177850, -0.0040720468], [1.9779984951, -2.4285922050, 0.4505937099],
                [0.0259040371, 0.7827717662, -0.8086757660]])
_M2i = np.linalg.inv(_M2)
_M1i = np.linalg.inv(_M1)


def hexrgb(h):
    h = h.lstrip('#')
    return tuple(int(h[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (0, 2, 4))


def to_lab(c):
    if isinstance(c, str):
        c = hexrgb(c)
    return _M2 @ np.cbrt(_M1 @ _lin(c))


def to_rgb(lab):
    lms = (_M2i @ np.asarray(lab, float)) ** 3
    return tuple(int(round(v)) for v in _srgb(_M1i @ lms))


def lab_img(rgb):
    """HxWx3 uint8 -> HxWx3 OKLab."""
    l = _lin(rgb.reshape(-1, 3))
    return (np.cbrt(l @ _M1.T) @ _M2.T).reshape(rgb.shape)


def mix(a, b, t):
    return to_rgb(to_lab(a) * (1 - t) + to_lab(b) * t)


def up(img, k):
    return np.repeat(np.repeat(img, k, 0), k, 1)


def save(img, path, k=1):
    Image.fromarray(up(np.asarray(img, np.uint8), k)).save(path)


_FONTS = {}


def font(sz=12):
    if sz not in _FONTS:
        for p in ["/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf", "/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc",
                  "/Library/Fonts/Arial.ttf"]:
            try:
                _FONTS[sz] = ImageFont.truetype(p, sz)
                break
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            _FONTS[sz] = ImageFont.load_default()
    return _FONTS[sz]


def grid(cells, cols, labels=None, rowlabels=None, collabels=None, pad=6, bg=(24, 24, 28), title=None,
         fs=12, rowlab_w=None):
    """cells: list of HxWx3 uint8 (already upscaled). Labels under each cell."""
    cw = max(c.shape[1] for c in cells)
    ch = max(c.shape[0] for c in cells)
    rows = (len(cells) + cols - 1) // cols
    lab_h = fs + 6 if labels else 0
    rl = rowlab_w if rowlab_w is not None else (max(len(s) for s in rowlabels) * fs * 0.6 + 10 if rowlabels else 0)
    rl = int(rl)
    top = (fs + 10 if collabels else 0) + (fs + 14 if title else 0)
    W = rl + cols * (cw + pad) + pad
    H = top + rows * (ch + lab_h + pad) + pad
    im = Image.new('RGB', (W, H), bg)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    y0 = pad
    if title:
        d.text((pad, pad), title, fill=(230, 230, 230), font=font(fs + 2))
        y0 += fs + 14
    if collabels:
        for i, s in enumerate(collabels):
            d.text((rl + pad + i * (cw + pad), y0), s, fill=(200, 200, 200), font=font(fs))
    for i, c in enumerate(cells):
        r, q = divmod(i, cols)
        x = rl + pad + q * (cw + pad)
        y = top + pad + r * (ch + lab_h + pad)
        im.paste(Image.fromarray(np.asarray(c, np.uint8)), (x, y))
        if labels and labels[i]:
            d.text((x, y + ch + 2), labels[i], fill=(190, 190, 190), font=font(fs))
    if rowlabels:
        for r, s in enumerate(rowlabels):
            d.text((pad, top + pad + r * (ch + lab_h + pad) + ch // 2 - fs // 2), s, fill=(200, 200, 200),
                   font=font(fs))
    return np.asarray(im)


def side(panels, labels=None, height=None, pad=8, bg=(24, 24, 28), fs=13):
    ps = []
    for p in panels:
        p = np.asarray(p, np.uint8)
        if height and p.shape[0] != height:
            k = height / p.shape[0]
            p = np.asarray(Image.fromarray(p).resize((max(1, int(p.shape[1] * k)), height), Image.NEAREST))
        ps.append(p)
    H = max(p.shape[0] for p in ps) + (fs + 8 if labels else 0) + 2 * pad
    W = sum(p.shape[1] for p in ps) + pad * (len(ps) + 1)
    im = Image.new('RGB', (W, H), bg)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    x = pad
    for i, p in enumerate(ps):
        im.paste(Image.fromarray(p), (x, pad))
        if labels:
            d.text((x, pad + p.shape[0] + 3), labels[i], fill=(200, 200, 200), font=font(fs))
        x += p.shape[1] + pad
    return np.asarray(im)


def vstack(imgs, pad=8, bg=(24, 24, 28)):
    W = max(i.shape[1] for i in imgs)
    out = []
    for i in imgs:
        if i.shape[1] < W:
            i = np.concatenate([i, np.full((i.shape[0], W - i.shape[1], 3), bg, np.uint8)], 1)
        out.append(i)
        out.append(np.full((pad, W, 3), bg, np.uint8))
    return np.concatenate(out[:-1], 0)
Write: elements/people/src/pal.py
"""Materials and palettes for people.

Index canvas convention: value = mat * 8 + step, step 0..4 (0 core/contact dark, 1 shadow,
2 halftone, 3 light, 4 highlight). 0 = empty.

Every ramp walks in OKLab from a cool dark (pulled toward the sky's ambient colour) to a warm
light (pulled toward the sun), as the willow and master-copy students found. Base colours come
from the naturalist's guide 06 (their suggestions from the materials, not measured) and from
looking at the Curtis photographs of bark garments.
"""
import numpy as np
from util import to_lab, to_rgb, hexrgb

NSTEP = 5
MATS = ['_', 'SKIN', 'HAIR', 'BARK', 'BARKT', 'HIDE', 'FUR', 'WOOL', 'BAND', 'STRAW', 'WOOD', 'STONE',
        'DARKB', 'FISH', 'SILVER', 'SHELL', 'LEAF', 'CEDAR', 'HIDED', 'NET', 'ROOT']
M = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(MATS)}

# (dark, mid, light) anchors per material; the ramp is built through these.
BASE = {
    'SKIN':  ('#3a2218', '#8a5a3e', '#c89468'),   # warm brown, sun-darkened
    'HAIR':  ('#0c0a0c', '#1c1a20', '#3c3a46'),   # black, blue-grey sheen
    'BARK':  ('#3a2216', '#8a5a3c', '#b8845a'),   # cedar bark, raw red-brown (06: #8A5A3C)
    'BARKT': ('#4a3620', '#9a7a4e', '#cfae78'),   # shredded / twined tan (06: #B08A5A)
    'HIDE':  ('#4a3422', '#a8865e', '#dcc096'),   # buckskin (06: #C9A77C)
    'HIDED': ('#2e2016', '#6b5038', '#9a7a58'),   # smoked hide (06: #8B6B4A)
    'FUR':   ('#2a241e', '#6a5e50', '#a0927c'),   # grey-brown fur robe
    'WOOL':  ('#6a6258', '#c8bea6', '#f0e8d2'),   # cream wool blanket (06: #E8E0C8)
    'BAND':  ('#3a1a12', '#7a3a26', '#a8583a'),   # alder-dyed red-brown band
    'DARKB': ('#141214', '#2a2626', '#4a4440'),   # black band
    'STRAW': ('#4a4028', '#a8965c', '#dccc8e'),   # cattail, bear grass, basket (06: #C8B57A)
    'WOOD':  ('#2a1c14', '#6a4a30', '#a07850'),
    'STONE': ('#28282c', '#5c5c60', '#96948e'),
    'FISH':  ('#4a1410', '#a8382a', '#e07058'),   # salmon flesh
    'SILVER':('#303a44', '#8a969c', '#d8e0e0'),   # fish skin
    'SHELL': ('#6a665c', '#d8d2c0', '#fcf8ea'),   # dentalium
    'LEAF':  ('#142014', '#3a5a2a', '#7a9a4a'),
    'CEDAR': ('#5a2a1a', '#b86a48', '#e8a078'),   # fresh-split cedar, salmon-pink/orange
    'NET':   ('#2a2418', '#6a5c40', '#9a8a64'),   # nettle-fibre net, cordage
    'ROOT':  ('#3a2a1a', '#8a6a44', '#c0a070'),   # camas bulbs, roots
}

HOURS = {
    #            sun colour         ambient          key   sky (top, horizon)
    'noon':     dict(sun='#fff8e8', amb='#8aa4cc', key=1.0, sky=('#78a8e0', '#d4e4f0')),
    'morning':  dict(sun='#ffecc8', amb='#8898c8', key=0.95, sky=('#94bce8', '#e4e8ee')),
    'golden':   dict(sun='#ffc27a', amb='#6e6ea8', key=0.9, sky=('#7896d2', '#f8d4a8')),
    'overcast': dict(sun='#e0e4e4', amb='#98a0a8', key=0.88, sky=('#b4bac0', '#d6d8da')),
    'dusk':     dict(sun='#f0967a', amb='#504682', key=0.66, sky=('#46468c', '#eca08a')),
    'night':    dict(sun='#ff9a40', amb='#1e2440', key=0.55, sky=('#0e1224', '#1e2438')),  # "sun" = firelight
}


def ramp(mat, hour='noon', depth=0.0, haze=None):
    d, m, l = (to_lab(hexrgb(c)) for c in BASE[mat])
    h = HOURS[hour]
    sun, amb = to_lab(hexrgb(h['sun'])), to_lab(hexrgb(h['amb']))
    key = h['key']
    # five steps: core, shadow, halftone, light, highlight
    pts = [d * 0.85 + m * 0.15 - np.array([0.03, 0, 0]),  # core darker than anchor
           d * 0.35 + m * 0.65,
           m * 0.7 + l * 0.3,
           l,
           l + (l - m) * 0.35 + np.array([0.04, 0, 0])]
    out = []
    for i, c in enumerate(pts):
        t = i / (NSTEP - 1)
        warm = 0.22 * t ** 1.4
        cool = 0.30 * (1 - t) ** 2
        c = c * (1 - warm - cool) + sun * warm + amb * cool
        c = c.copy()
        c[0] *= key if i < 3 else (key * 0.6 + 0.4 * key ** 0.5)
        if depth > 0:
            hz = to_lab(hexrgb(haze or h['sky'][1]))
            # darks lift toward the haze a lot, lights barely (master-copy measurement)
            a = depth * (0.85 - 0.55 * t)
            c = c * (1 - a) + hz * a
        out.append(to_rgb(c))
    return out


def palette(hour='noon', depth=0.0, haze=None):
    """Return lut: (len(MATS)*8, 3) uint8."""
    lut = np.zeros((len(MATS) * 8, 3), np.uint8)
    for n in MATS[1:]:
        r = ramp(n, hour, depth, haze)
        for s in range(NSTEP):
            lut[M[n] * 8 + s] = r[s]
    return lut


def grey_lut():
    lut = palette('noon')
    lab = np.array([to_lab(c) for c in lut])
    g = np.array([to_rgb([L, 0, 0]) for L in lab[:, 0]])
    g[0] = 0
    return g.astype(np.uint8)
Write: elements/people/src/fig.py
"""Figures: a 3D posed skeleton, dressed in capsules, rasterized straight into an index canvas.

Units: an adult standing is ~1.0 tall. World axes x right, y up, z toward the viewer.
Camera: orthographic, elevation e (0 = eye level, 30 = the iso view). Screen up
su = y cos e - z sin e; depth toward viewer dz = z cos e + y sin e.

Everything is a capsule with a radius profile (limbs, torso halves, garments as larger
capsules that swallow the limbs inside them, props), z-buffered per pixel at the target
resolution, so a 6 px figure is rasterized as a 6 px figure, never downsampled. Narrow
forms (radius under ~0.7 px) are drawn as clean Bresenham runs of their axis, so a 1 px arm
or spear is a clean line rather than broken coverage.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from pal import M

D2R = math.pi / 180


def v3(*a):
    return np.array(a, float)


def nrm(v):
    n = np.linalg.norm(v)
    return v / n if n > 1e-9 else v


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# body proportions

def proportions(age='adult', sex='f', size_px=24):
    """Segment lengths in units of adult height. Small figures get a larger head (readability),
    as every sprite artist does; the head is ~1/7.5 of height in life, ~1/6 at 12 px, ~1/5 at 6 px."""
    P = dict(thigh=0.245, shin=0.235, ankle=0.035, torso=0.30, neck=0.04, head=0.13,
             sh=0.115, hip=0.08, uarm=0.17, farm=0.15, foot=0.075, stature=1.0,
             r_torso=0.068, r_thigh=0.052, r_shin=0.038, r_uarm=0.031, r_farm=0.026, girth=1.0)
    if sex == 'f':
        P.update(stature=0.94, sh=0.105, hip=0.085, r_uarm=0.028, r_farm=0.023)
    if sex == 'm':
        P.update(stature=1.0, sh=0.12, r_torso=0.072, r_uarm=0.033)
    if age == 'child':      # about 7-9 years: 4.5-5 heads
        P.update(stature=0.62, head=0.16, thigh=0.215, shin=0.2, torso=0.28, sh=0.10, hip=0.075, uarm=0.16,
                 farm=0.14, r_torso=0.07, r_thigh=0.05, r_uarm=0.03, r_farm=0.026)
    if age == 'small':      # about 3-4 years
        P.update(stature=0.48, head=0.2, thigh=0.19, shin=0.17, torso=0.27, sh=0.1, hip=0.08, uarm=0.15,
                 farm=0.13, r_torso=0.078, r_thigh=0.058, r_uarm=0.033, r_farm=0.03)
    if age == 'elder':
        P.update(stature=P['stature'] * 0.97)
    # head exaggeration at small pixel sizes (in figure-relative units)
    px = size_px
    boost = 1.0 + 0.45 * np.clip((20 - px) / 14, 0, 1)
    P['head'] *= boost
    # limbs thicken slightly at small sizes so they survive (they are drawn as >=1px anyway)
    return P


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# skeleton

JOINTS = ['pelvis', 'chest', 'neck', 'head', 'shL', 'shR', 'elL', 'elR', 'haL', 'haR',
          'hpL', 'hpR', 'knL', 'knR', 'anL', 'anR', 'toL', 'toR']


def default_pose():
    return dict(pitch=0.0, roll=0.0, twist=0.0, headp=0.0, headyaw=0.0,
                hipL=0.0, hipR=0.0, knL=0.0, knR=0.0, habL=3.0, habR=3.0,
                shL=0.0, shR=0.0, elL=8.0, elR=8.0, sabL=6.0, sabR=6.0,
                footL=0.0, footR=0.0, seat=None, bob=0.0, dx=0.0, ground=True)


def fk(pose, P):
    """Forward kinematics in the body frame (f forward, y up, l body's left). Returns dict of
    3D points (f, y, l) with the lowest ground contact at y = 0 (or pelvis on the seat)."""
    p = default_pose()
    p.update(pose)
    pit = p['pitch'] * D2R
    roll = p['roll'] * D2R
    twist = p['twist'] * D2R
    up = v3(math.sin(pit), math.cos(pit), 0)            # trunk axis
    fw = v3(math.cos(pit), -math.sin(pit), 0)           # trunk forward
    lat = v3(0, 0, 1)
    # roll: lean sideways (rotate up/lat about fw)
    if roll:
        up = nrm(up * math.cos(roll) + lat * math.sin(roll))
        lat = nrm(np.cross(fw, up)) * -1 if False else nrm(lat * math.cos(roll) - v3(*(up * 0)) - v3(0, 1, 0) * math.sin(roll) * 0 + v3(0, -math.sin(roll), 0))
    J = {}
    pel = v3(p['dx'], 0, 0)
    J['pelvis'] = pel
    chest = pel + up * P['torso']
    J['chest'] = chest
    hp = p['headp'] * D2R + pit * 0.3
    hup = v3(math.sin(hp), math.cos(hp), 0)
    J['neck'] = chest + nrm(up + hup) * P['neck']
    J['head'] = J['neck'] + hup * P['head'] * 0.5
    J['headup'] = hup
    # shoulders with twist about the trunk axis
    tl = lat * math.cos(twist) + fw * math.sin(twist)
    tf = fw * math.cos(twist) - lat * math.sin(twist)
    for s, sg in (('L', 1), ('R', -1)):
        sh = chest - up * 0.02 + tl * P['sh'] * sg
        J['sh' + s] = sh
        a = p['sh' + s] * D2R
        b = p['sab' + s] * D2R
        c = p['el' + s] * D2R
        d = -up * math.cos(a) + tf * math.sin(a)
        perp = up * math.sin(a) + tf * math.cos(a)
        d1 = nrm(d * math.cos(b) + tl * sg * math.sin(b))
        el = sh + d1 * P['uarm']
        d2 = nrm(d1 * math.cos(c) + perp * math.sin(c))
        J['el' + s] = el
        J['ha' + s] = el + d2 * P['farm']
    # legs relative to world vertical
    for s, sg in (('L', 1), ('R', -1)):
        hj = pel + lat * P['hip'] * sg
        J['hp' + s] = hj
        h = p['hip' + s] * D2R
        k = p['kn' + s] * D2R
        ab = p['hab' + s] * D2R
        td = nrm(v3(math.sin(h), -math.cos(h), 0) * math.cos(ab) + lat * sg * math.sin(ab))
        kn = hj + td * P['thigh']
        sd = nrm(v3(math.sin(h - k), -math.cos(h - k), 0) * math.cos(ab) + lat * sg * math.sin(ab) * 0.5)
        an = kn + sd * P['shin']
        J['kn' + s] = kn
        J['an' + s] = an
        fa = p['foot' + s] * D2R     # foot pitch (toe down positive)
        J['to' + s] = an + v3(math.cos(fa), -math.sin(fa) - 0.25, 0) * P['foot']
    # grounding
    if p['seat'] is not None:
        shift = p['seat'] + 0.05 - J['pelvis'][1]
    else:
        cands = [J['anL'][1] - P['ankle'], J['anR'][1] - P['ankle'], J['toL'][1] - 0.012, J['toR'][1] - 0.012,
                 J['knL'][1] - 0.04, J['knR'][1] - 0.04, J['pelvis'][1] - 0.07]
        shift = -min(cands)
    shift += p['bob']
    for k in list(J):
        if k != 'headup':
            J[k] = J[k] + v3(0, shift, 0)
    J['_up'] = up
    J['_fw'] = fw
    J['_lat'] = tl
    return J


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# primitives

class Prim:
    __slots__ = ('a', 'b', 'r0', 'r1', 'mat', 'prof', 'caps', 'side', 'tag', 'clip', 'stepmod', 'thin', 'bias',
                 'pattern', 'minw')

    def __init__(self, a, b, r0, r1=None, mat='SKIN', prof='lin', caps=True, side=0, tag='', clip=None,
                 stepmod=0, thin=True, bias=0.0, pattern=None, minw=1):
        self.a, self.b = np.asarray(a, float), np.asarray(b, float)
        self.r0 = r0
        self.r1 = r0 if r1 is None else r1
        self.mat, self.prof, self.caps, self.side, self.tag = mat, prof, caps, side, tag
        self.clip, self.stepmod, self.thin, self.bias, self.pattern, self.minw = clip, stepmod, thin, bias, pattern, minw


def to_world(pt, facing, origin):
    """body frame (f, y, l) -> world (x, y, z). facing in degrees: 0 = facing screen right,
    90 = facing the viewer, 180 = left, 270 = away."""
    th = facing * D2R
    f = v3(math.cos(th), 0, math.sin(th))
    l = v3(math.sin(th), 0, -math.cos(th))
    return origin + f * pt[0] + v3(0, pt[1], 0) + l * pt[2]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# rasterizer

class Canvas:
    """An index canvas with z-buffer and normal buffer, in pixels (y down)."""

    def __init__(self, w, h, elev=10.0):
        self.w, self.h = w, h
        self.idx = np.zeros((h, w), np.int16)
        self.z = np.full((h, w), -1e9)
        self.n = np.zeros((h, w, 3))
        self.part = np.full((h, w), -1, np.int32)
        self.stepmod = np.zeros((h, w), np.int8)
        self.fig = np.full((h, w), -1, np.int32)
        self.pat = np.zeros((h, w), np.float32)
        self.e = elev * D2R
        self.nparts = 0

    def project(self, P, ox, oy, scale):
        """world point -> (sx, sy, depth) in pixels."""
        ce, se = math.cos(self.e), math.sin(self.e)
        su = P[1] * ce - P[2] * se
        dz = P[2] * ce + P[1] * se
        return np.array([ox + P[0] * scale, oy - su * scale, dz * scale])

    def unproject_dir(self, v):
        """camera-space normal (x right, y up(screen), z toward viewer) -> world."""
        ce, se = math.cos(self.e), math.sin(self.e)
        x, yu, zc = v[..., 0], v[..., 1], v[..., 2]
        wy = yu * ce + zc * se
        wz = -yu * se + zc * ce
        return np.stack([x, wy, wz], -1)

    def draw(self, prim, ox, oy, scale, figid=0):
        A = self.project(prim.a, ox, oy, scale)
        B = self.project(prim.b, ox, oy, scale)
        r0, r1 = prim.r0 * scale, prim.r1 * scale
        rm = max(r0, r1)
        pid = self.nparts
        self.nparts += 1
        x0 = int(math.floor(min(A[0], B[0]) - rm - 1))
        x1 = int(math.ceil(max(A[0], B[0]) + rm + 1))
        y0 = int(math.floor(min(A[1], B[1]) - rm - 1))
        y1 = int(math.ceil(max(A[1], B[1]) + rm + 1))
        x0, y0 = max(x0, 0), max(y0, 0)
        x1, y1 = min(x1, self.w - 1), min(y1, self.h - 1)
        if x1 < x0 or y1 < y0:
            return
        ys, xs = np.mgrid[y0:y1 + 1, x0:x1 + 1]
        px, py = xs + 0.5, ys + 0.5
        d = B[:2] - A[:2]
        L2 = d @ d
        if L2 < 1e-9:
            t = np.zeros_like(px)
        else:
            t = ((px - A[0]) * d[0] + (py - A[1]) * d[1]) / L2
        tc = np.clip(t, 0, 1)
        if prim.prof == 'spindle':
            r = r0 + (r1 - r0) * np.sin(np.pi * tc) ** 0.7
        elif prim.prof == 'bell':      # flares near the far end (capes, skirts)
            r = r0 + (r1 - r0) * tc ** 0.7
        else:
            r = r0 + (r1 - r0) * tc
        cx = A[0] + d[0] * tc
        cy = A[1] + d[1] * tc
        ox_, oy_ = px - cx, py - cy
        dist2 = ox_ ** 2 + oy_ ** 2
        inside = dist2 <= (r + prim.bias) ** 2
        if not prim.caps:
            inside &= (t >= -0.02) & (t <= 1.02)
        # thin forms: a clean Bresenham run of the axis
        thin_mask = np.zeros_like(inside)
        if prim.thin and rm < 0.75:
            for (lx, ly) in bresenham(A[0], A[1], B[0], B[1]):
                if y0 <= ly <= y1 and x0 <= lx <= x1:
                    thin_mask[ly - y0, lx - x0] = True
            inside |= thin_mask
        if prim.clip is not None:
            inside &= prim.clip(xs, ys)
        if not inside.any():
            return
        rr = np.maximum(r, 0.5)
        zc2 = np.maximum(rr ** 2 - dist2, 0)
        zs = np.sqrt(zc2)
        depth = A[2] + (B[2] - A[2]) * tc + zs
        # camera-space normal
        nx = ox_ / rr
        ny = -oy_ / rr
        nz = zs / rr
        # thin lines get a normal facing across the axis toward the light-neutral viewer
        nn = np.stack([nx, ny, nz], -1)
        ln = np.linalg.norm(nn, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
        nn = np.where(ln > 1e-6, nn / np.maximum(ln, 1e-6), np.array([0, 0, 1.0]))
        wn = self.unproject_dir(nn)
        sub = (slice(y0, y1 + 1), slice(x0, x1 + 1))
        win = inside & (depth > self.z[sub])
        if not win.any():
            return
        from pal import M as _M
        self.z[sub][win] = depth[win]
        self.idx[sub][win] = _M[prim.mat] * 8 + 2
        self.n[sub][win] = wn[win]
        self.part[sub][win] = pid
        self.stepmod[sub][win] = prim.stepmod + (-1 if prim.side < 0 else 0)
        self.fig[sub][win] = figid
        if prim.pattern is not None:
            self.pat[sub][win] = prim.pattern(tc, xs, ys)[win]
        else:
            self.pat[sub][win] = 0


def bresenham(x0, y0, x1, y1):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = int(math.floor(x0)), int(math.floor(y0)), int(math.floor(x1)), int(math.floor(y1))
    dx, dy = abs(x1 - x0), -abs(y1 - y0)
    sx, sy = (1 if x0 < x1 else -1), (1 if y0 < y1 else -1)
    err = dx + dy
    out = []
    while True:
        out.append((x0, y0))
        if x0 == x1 and y0 == y1:
            break
        e2 = 2 * err
        if e2 >= dy:
            err += dy
            x0 += sx
        if e2 <= dx:
            err += dx
            y0 += sy
    return out

Write: elements/people/src/poses.py
"""Activities as keyframed joint angles, phase in [0, 1).

Angles in degrees. hip: thigh forward of vertical (+). kn: knee flexion (+). sh: arm raised
forward from hanging (+), sab: arm out sideways, el: elbow flexion. pitch: trunk bent forward.
headp: head pitched down (+). seat: pelvis rests at this height (units). bob: vertical offset.

Sources for the poses: Muybridge's walking, carrying and lifting plates (weight shift, the
counterweight lean under a load), Bruegel's Harvesters (the binder bent from the hips, the
eaters sitting with knees up), Millet's Gleaners (the bend with one straight leg), the Curtis
Clayoquot fisherman standing in a canoe with a spear, and the naturalist's descriptions of each
task (digging stick with both hands, maul on wedge, tumpline across the forehead).
"""
import numpy as np


def smooth(t):
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)


def interp(keys, phase):
    """keys: list of (phase, dict). Cyclic, smoothstep between keys."""
    phase = phase % 1.0
    ks = sorted(keys, key=lambda k: k[0])
    for i in range(len(ks)):
        p0, a = ks[i]
        p1, b = ks[(i + 1) % len(ks)]
        if p1 <= p0:
            p1 += 1.0
        ph = phase if phase >= p0 else phase + 1.0
        if p0 <= ph < p1:
            t = smooth((ph - p0) / (p1 - p0))
            out = {}
            for k in set(a) | set(b):
                va, vb = a.get(k, b.get(k)), b.get(k, a.get(k))
                if va is None or vb is None or isinstance(va, bool):
                    out[k] = va if t < 0.5 else vb
                else:
                    out[k] = va + (vb - va) * t
            return out
    return dict(ks[0][1])


def mirror(d):
    out = {}
    for k, v in d.items():
        if k.endswith('L'):
            out[k[:-1] + 'R'] = v
        elif k.endswith('R'):
            out[k[:-1] + 'L'] = v
        else:
            out[k] = v
    return out


# walk half-cycle (Muybridge plate: contact, down, passing, up), mirrored for the second half
WALK = [
    (0.0, dict(hipL=24, knL=4, hipR=-16, knR=12, footR=18, shL=-16, shR=18, elL=10, elR=18, bob=-0.004)),
    (0.125, dict(hipL=16, knL=20, hipR=-20, knR=28, footR=35, shL=-12, shR=14, elL=10, elR=16, bob=-0.014)),
    (0.25, dict(hipL=2, knL=8, hipR=18, knR=62, footR=10, shL=0, shR=0, elL=10, elR=12, bob=0.004)),
    (0.375, dict(hipL=-14, knL=4, hipR=28, knR=30, footL=12, shL=12, shR=-12, elL=14, elR=10, bob=0.012)),
]


def cycle(half, phase):
    keys = list(half) + [(p + 0.5, mirror(d)) for p, d in half]
    return interp(keys, phase)


RUN = [
    (0.0, dict(hipL=40, knL=20, hipR=-30, knR=50, footR=30, shL=-40, shR=45, elL=80, elR=90, pitch=12, bob=0.0)),
    (0.2, dict(hipL=10, knL=35, hipR=10, knR=100, shL=-10, shR=20, elL=85, elR=90, pitch=12, bob=-0.02)),
    (0.35, dict(hipL=-25, knL=15, hipR=50, knR=60, shL=30, shR=-30, elL=90, elR=80, pitch=12, bob=0.05)),
]


def pose(activity, phase=0.0, age='adult', sex='f'):
    a = activity
    if a == 'stand':
        br = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(phase * 2 * np.pi)
        return dict(hipL=2, hipR=-2, knL=3, knR=3, shL=-3 + br * 2, shR=4, elL=12, elR=10, sabL=7, sabR=7,
                    headp=4)
    if a == 'walk':
        return cycle(WALK, phase)
    if a == 'carry':          # tumpline basket: forward lean from the ankles, short steps, hands at the strap
        p = cycle([(k, {**d, 'hipL': d['hipL'] * 0.8, 'hipR': d['hipR'] * 0.8}) for k, d in WALK], phase)
        p.update(pitch=20, headp=-8, shL=62, shR=58, elL=112, elR=118, sabL=14, sabR=14)
        p['hipL'] += 14
        p['hipR'] += 14
        return p
    if a == 'carry_load':     # firewood or bundle on the shoulder, one hand steadying
        p = cycle(WALK, phase)
        p.update(pitch=8, shR=150, elR=150, sabR=10, headp=6)
        p['hipL'] += 5
        p['hipR'] += 5
        return p
    if a == 'dig':            # digging stick: bent from the hips, both hands on the stick; lift then thrust
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=62, headp=10, hipL=38, hipR=20, knL=26, knR=14, shL=32, shR=46, elL=40, elR=50,
                           sabL=8, sabR=4)),
                (0.45, dict(pitch=56, headp=5, hipL=36, hipR=18, knL=22, knR=12, shL=66, shR=80, elL=70, elR=74,
                            sabL=8, sabR=4)),
                (0.6, dict(pitch=66, headp=12, hipL=40, hipR=22, knL=30, knR=16, shL=18, shR=28, elL=24, elR=30,
                           sabL=8, sabR=4))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'gather':         # kneeling on one knee, one hand to the ground, one to the basket
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=38, headp=18, hipL=95, knL=95, hipR=10, knR=100, shR=70, elR=10, shL=30, elL=40,
                           sabL=20)),
                (0.5, dict(pitch=46, headp=22, hipL=95, knL=98, hipR=12, knR=104, shR=82, elR=6, shL=28, elL=46,
                           sabL=20))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'split':          # stone maul overhead onto an antler wedge; feet apart, strike from the hips
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=10, headp=15, hipL=16, hipR=-12, knL=14, knR=6, shL=178, shR=172, elL=40, elR=46,
                           sabL=2, sabR=2)),
                (0.35, dict(pitch=16, headp=15, hipL=16, hipR=-12, knL=14, knR=6, shL=190, shR=186, elL=58, elR=62,
                            sabL=2, sabR=2)),
                (0.55, dict(pitch=44, headp=30, hipL=22, hipR=-6, knL=26, knR=16, shL=58, shR=52, elL=10, elR=12,
                            sabL=2, sabR=2)),
                (0.75, dict(pitch=36, headp=26, hipL=20, hipR=-8, knL=22, knR=12, shL=60, shR=54, elL=12, elR=14,
                            sabL=2, sabR=2))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'weave':          # sitting back on the heels on a mat, hands working at the lap
        w = np.sin(phase * 2 * np.pi)
        return dict(pitch=14, headp=28, hipL=92, hipR=92, knL=168, knR=168, footL=-60, footR=-60,
                    shL=36 + 6 * w, shR=40 - 6 * w, elL=82, elR=76, sabL=10, sabR=10)
    if a == 'fire':           # crouched at a hearth, one arm feeding a stick
        w = smooth(0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(phase * 2 * np.pi))
        return dict(pitch=32, headp=18, hipL=112, hipR=100, knL=140, knR=128, footL=-10, footR=-10,
                    shR=58 + 18 * w, elR=18 - 8 * w, shL=26, elL=74, sabL=14)
    if a == 'sit':            # on the ground, knees up, arms loosely round the knees
        return dict(seat=0.0, pitch=8, headp=6, hipL=72, hipR=66, knL=118, knR=112, footL=-20, footR=-20,
                    shL=42, shR=40, elL=48, elR=44, sabL=6, sabR=6)
    if a == 'sit_log':        # on a log, forearms on knees, turned toward the fire
        return dict(seat=0.22, pitch=26, headp=10, hipL=88, hipR=84, knL=92, knR=86, shL=38, shR=34, elL=52,
                    elR=58, sabL=4, sabR=4)
    if a == 'sit_eat':        # sitting cross-legged-ish, a bowl to the mouth (Bruegel's eaters)
        return dict(seat=0.0, pitch=12, headp=10, hipL=84, hipR=80, knL=150, knR=146, habL=40, habR=40,
                    shL=55, shR=62, elL=110, elR=118, sabL=8, sabR=8)
    if a == 'spear':          # standing fisherman, spear held high, point down (Curtis, Clayoquot fisherman)
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=18, headp=30, hipL=10, hipR=-8, knL=12, knR=8, shR=140, elR=70, shL=58, elL=26,
                           sabL=6, sabR=10)),
                (0.5, dict(pitch=30, headp=34, hipL=14, hipR=-6, knL=18, knR=10, shR=90, elR=16, shL=44, elL=10,
                           sabL=6, sabR=10))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'dipnet':         # long dip-net pole, swept downstream
        w = phase
        return dict(pitch=26, headp=24, hipL=18, hipR=-10, knL=20, knR=10, shL=48 + 10 * np.sin(w * 6.28),
                    shR=76 + 10 * np.sin(w * 6.28), elL=20, elR=40, sabL=4, sabR=4)
    if a == 'paddle':         # kneeling in a canoe; top hand over the grip, lower hand low on the shaft
        keys = [(0.0, dict(seat=0.0, pitch=18, headp=0, hipL=92, hipR=92, knL=168, knR=168, footL=-60,
                           footR=-60, shL=100, elL=80, shR=62, elR=14, sabL=14, sabR=24, twist=18)),
                (0.5, dict(seat=0.0, pitch=34, headp=6, hipL=92, hipR=92, knL=168, knR=168, footL=-60,
                           footR=-60, shL=62, elL=40, shR=18, elR=10, sabL=14, sabR=24, twist=-12))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'pound':          # kneeling, stone pestle in both hands
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=24, headp=24, hipL=92, hipR=92, knL=168, knR=168, footL=-60, footR=-60, shL=88,
                           shR=92, elL=70, elR=70, sabL=6, sabR=6)),
                (0.5, dict(pitch=34, headp=26, hipL=92, hipR=92, knL=168, knR=168, footL=-60, footR=-60, shL=40,
                           shR=44, elL=40, elR=40, sabL=6, sabR=6))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'run':
        return cycle(RUN, phase)
    if a == 'squat_play':     # a child squatting over stones or shells, one arm reaching
        w = np.sin(phase * 2 * np.pi)
        return dict(pitch=34, headp=24, hipL=118, hipR=112, knL=150, knR=146, footL=-10, footR=-10,
                    shR=50 + 20 * w, elR=10, shL=30, elL=50, sabL=10)
    if a == 'throw':          # a child throwing (hoop-and-pole, stones into the river)
        keys = [(0.0, dict(pitch=-6, hipL=20, hipR=-20, knL=10, knR=10, shR=160, elR=60, shL=40, elL=10,
                           twist=-25)),
                (0.4, dict(pitch=20, hipL=24, hipR=-10, knL=20, knR=6, shR=40, elR=10, shL=-20, elL=10, twist=20))]
        return interp(keys, phase)
    if a == 'staff':          # an elder walking slowly with a staff
        p = cycle([(k, {**d, 'hipL': d['hipL'] * 0.6, 'hipR': d['hipR'] * 0.6}) for k, d in WALK], phase)
        p.update(pitch=14, headp=6, shR=34, elR=40, sabR=8, shL=-4, elL=20)
        return p
    if a == 'hold_baby':      # standing, a baby on the cradleboard held in front
        return dict(pitch=-4, headp=22, hipL=4, hipR=-2, knL=4, knR=4, shL=28, shR=24, elL=100, elR=96,
                    sabL=10, sabR=10)
    raise ValueError(a)


ACTIVITIES = ['stand', 'walk', 'carry', 'carry_load', 'dig', 'gather', 'split', 'weave', 'fire', 'sit', 'sit_log',
              'sit_eat', 'spear', 'dipnet', 'paddle', 'pound', 'run', 'squat_play', 'throw', 'staff', 'hold_baby']

Write: elements/people/src/person.py
"""A person: spec -> posed, dressed, propped capsules -> rendered index sprite.

spec keys (all optional):
  activity, phase, sex ('f'/'m'), age ('adult','elder','child','small'), dress (see DRESS),
  hair ('loose','tied','queues','short'), facing (deg), seed, prop overrides.

Dress is from the naturalist's guide 06 (Haeberlin & Gunther 1930, Eells 1877, Spier & Sapir 1930),
pre-horse, AD ~1400-1730:
  coast_f_summer   knee-length shredded cedar-bark skirt, bare above
  coast_f_rain     the skirt + twined cedar-bark cape to the hips + wide-brimmed twined hat
  coast_f_hide     (well-to-do) buckskin shirt to below the knee
  coast_f_wool     skirt + cream wool blanket over the shoulders
  coast_m_summer   breechclout
  coast_m_blanket  breechclout + wool blanket over the shoulders
  coast_m_fur      breechclout + fur robe
  coast_m_hide     (well-to-do, winter) fringed buckskin shirt to the hips + leggings
  coast_m_rain     cape + hat
  plateau_f        fringed skin apron-skirt, basket hat, (robe in cold)
  plateau_f_robe   the same with a fur robe
  plateau_m        breechclout, hair in two queues in front
  plateau_m_robe   the same with a fur robe
  child_bare       (summer) nothing
  child_robe       a small skin robe
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from fig import (Prim, Canvas, fk, proportions, to_world, v3, nrm, D2R)
from poses import pose as get_pose
from pal import M

DRESS = ['coast_f_summer', 'coast_f_rain', 'coast_f_hide', 'coast_f_wool', 'coast_m_summer', 'coast_m_blanket',
         'coast_m_fur', 'coast_m_hide', 'coast_m_rain', 'plateau_f', 'plateau_f_robe', 'plateau_m',
         'plateau_m_robe', 'child_bare', 'child_robe']


def hsh(*a):
    h = 2166136261
    for x in a:
        h = ((h ^ (int(x) & 0xffffffff)) * 16777619) & 0xffffffff
    return h


def default_dress(sex, age, region='coast'):
    if age in ('child', 'small'):
        return 'child_bare'
    if region == 'plateau':
        return 'plateau_f' if sex == 'f' else 'plateau_m'
    return 'coast_f_summer' if sex == 'f' else 'coast_m_summer'


def default_prop(activity):
    return {'dig': 'digstick', 'carry': 'basket_back', 'carry_load': 'firewood', 'split': 'maul', 'spear': 'spear',
            'dipnet': 'dipnet', 'paddle': 'paddle', 'pound': 'pestle', 'staff': 'staff', 'hold_baby': 'cradle',
            'fire': 'firestick', 'gather': 'basket_hip', 'weave': 'weaving', 'sit_eat': 'bowl',
            'squat_play': None, 'throw': None}.get(activity)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def build(spec, size_px):
    """Return list of Prims in world coordinates (units), and the joints (world)."""
    act = spec.get('activity', 'stand')
    sex = spec.get('sex', 'f')
    age = spec.get('age', 'adult')
    region = spec.get('region', 'coast')
    dress = spec.get('dress') or default_dress(sex, age, region)
    facing = spec.get('facing', 0.0)
    seed = spec.get('seed', 0)
    ph = spec.get('phase', 0.0)
    P = proportions(age, sex, size_px * (1.0 if age == 'adult' else 0.8))
    st = P['stature']
    pz = get_pose(act, ph, age, sex)
    pz.update(spec.get('pose_over', {}))
    if age == 'elder':
        pz['pitch'] = pz.get('pitch', 0) + 8
        pz['headp'] = pz.get('headp', 0) + 4
    # scale segment lengths by stature
    Ps = dict(P)
    for k in ('thigh', 'shin', 'ankle', 'torso', 'neck', 'sh', 'hip', 'uarm', 'farm', 'foot', 'r_torso', 'r_thigh',
              'r_shin', 'r_uarm', 'r_farm'):
        Ps[k] = P[k] * st
    Ps['head'] = P['head'] * st
    if age in ('child', 'small'):
        for k in ('thigh', 'shin', 'torso', 'uarm', 'farm', 'sh', 'hip', 'r_torso', 'r_thigh', 'r_shin', 'r_uarm',
                  'r_farm'):
            Ps[k] = P[k] * st / 0.62 * 0.62 if age == 'child' else P[k] * st
        # child proportions already given in adult units relative to their own stature
        scale = st / (P['thigh'] + P['shin'] + P['torso'] + P['neck'] + P['head'] + P['ankle'])
        for k in ('thigh', 'shin', 'ankle', 'torso', 'neck', 'head', 'sh', 'hip', 'uarm', 'farm', 'foot'):
            Ps[k] = P[k] * scale
        for k in ('r_torso', 'r_thigh', 'r_shin', 'r_uarm', 'r_farm'):
            Ps[k] = P[k] * scale
    J = fk(pz, Ps)
    origin = v3(spec.get('x', 0.0), 0, spec.get('z', 0.0))
    W = lambda p: to_world(p, facing, origin)
    th = facing * D2R
    lz = -math.cos(th)                           # z of body's left in world
    sideL = -1 if lz < -0.35 else (1 if lz > 0.35 else 0)
    sideR = -sideL
    side = {'L': sideL, 'R': sideR}
    up, fw, lat = J['_up'], J['_fw'], J['_lat']
    prims = []

    def add(a, b, r0, r1=None, mat='SKIN', **kw):
        prims.append(Prim(W(a), W(b), r0, r1, mat, **kw))

    hide_shirt = dress in ('coast_m_hide', 'coast_f_hide')
    torso_mat = 'HIDE' if hide_shirt else 'SKIN'
    # --- legs
    leg_mat = 'HIDE' if dress == 'coast_m_hide' else 'SKIN'
    for s in 'LR':
        add(J['hp' + s], J['kn' + s], Ps['r_thigh'], Ps['r_thigh'] * 0.8, leg_mat, side=side[s], tag='thigh' + s)
        add(J['kn' + s], J['an' + s], Ps['r_shin'], Ps['r_shin'] * 0.72, leg_mat, side=side[s], tag='shin' + s)
        add(J['an' + s], J['to' + s], Ps['r_shin'] * 0.6, Ps['r_shin'] * 0.5, 'SKIN', side=side[s], tag='foot' + s)
    # --- torso (two halves, so the profile is narrower than the front view)
    tw = Ps['r_torso']
    hipw = Ps['hip'] * 0.62
    shw = Ps['sh'] * 0.5
    for sg in (1, -1):
        add(J['pelvis'] + lat * hipw * sg + up * 0.02, J['chest'] + lat * shw * sg - up * 0.03, tw * 1.02, tw,
            torso_mat, tag='torso')
    add(J['hpL'] + up * 0.03, J['hpR'] + up * 0.03, tw * 1.05, None, torso_mat, tag='pelvis')
    add(J['chest'] - up * 0.02, J['neck'], Ps['r_uarm'] * 1.0, None, 'SKIN', tag='neck')
    # --- head and hair
    hr = Ps['head'] * 0.5
    hu = J['headup']
    hc = J['head']
    hfw = nrm(v3(hu[1], -hu[0], 0))             # head forward
    add(hc, hc, hr, None, 'SKIN', tag='head')
    hair = spec.get('hair') or ('loose' if sex == 'f' else ('tied' if region == 'coast' else 'queues'))
    if age == 'small':
        hair = 'short'
    add(hc - hfw * hr * 0.28 + hu * hr * 0.14, hc - hfw * hr * 0.28 + hu * hr * 0.14, hr * 0.98, None, 'HAIR',
        tag='hair')
    if hair == 'loose':
        add(hc - hfw * hr * 0.55, J['chest'] - fw * tw * 0.9 - up * 0.10, hr * 0.62, hr * 0.7, 'HAIR', tag='hairlong')
    elif hair == 'tied':
        add(hc - hfw * hr * 0.8, hc - hfw * hr * 1.1 - hu * hr * 0.5, hr * 0.42, None, 'HAIR', tag='hairknot')
    elif hair == 'queues':
        for sg in (1, -1):
            a = hc + lat * hr * 0.75 * sg - hu * hr * 0.3
            add(a, J['chest'] + lat * shw * 0.9 * sg + fw * tw * 0.9 - up * 0.08, hr * 0.2, hr * 0.18, 'HAIR',
                tag='queue', side=side['L' if sg > 0 else 'R'])
    # --- arms (drawn after torso; z-buffer resolves which is in front)
    arm_mat = 'HIDE' if hide_shirt else 'SKIN'
    for s in 'LR':
        add(J['sh' + s], J['el' + s], Ps['r_uarm'], Ps['r_uarm'] * 0.85, arm_mat, side=side[s], tag='uarm' + s)
        add(J['el' + s], J['ha' + s], Ps['r_farm'], Ps['r_farm'] * 0.8, arm_mat if dress != 'coast_f_hide' else 'SKIN',
            side=side[s], tag='farm' + s)
        hd = nrm(J['ha' + s] - J['el' + s])
        add(J['ha' + s], J['ha' + s] + hd * 0.035 * st, Ps['r_farm'] * 0.85, Ps['r_farm'] * 0.7, 'SKIN',
            side=side[s], tag='hand' + s)
    # --- garments
    knee_mid = (J['knL'] + J['knR']) / 2
    waist = J['pelvis'] + up * 0.07
    rng = np.random.default_rng(hsh(seed, 7))

    def skirt(mat, length=1.0, rtop=0.105, rbot=0.125, fringe=True):
        # the hem hangs from the knees (or over the thighs when sitting)
        kn = knee_mid
        hem = waist + (kn - waist) * length
        hem = hem + v3(0, -0.04 * length, 0)
        spread = np.linalg.norm((J['knL'] - J['knR'])[[0, 2]]) * 0.5
        add(waist, hem, rtop * st, max(rbot * st, spread + 0.06 * st), mat, prof='lin', caps=False, tag='skirt',
            pattern=(lambda tc, xs, ys: (tc > 0.72) * 1.0) if fringe else None)

    def cape(mat, length=1.0, r1=0.19, tag='cape', pattern=None):
        top = J['neck'] - up * 0.005
        bot = J['pelvis'] + up * (0.02 + (1 - length) * 0.25) - up * max(0, length - 1) * 0.45
        add(top, bot, 0.07 * st, r1 * st, mat, prof='bell', caps=False, tag=tag, pattern=pattern)

    def blanket(mat, length=1.25, pattern=None):
        top = J['neck'] - up * 0.01
        bot = J['pelvis'] - up * (length - 1.0) * 0.55 + fw * 0.02
        add(top, bot, 0.085 * st, 0.17 * st, mat, prof='bell', caps=False, tag='blanket', pattern=pattern)

    def bands(tc, xs, ys):
        return np.where((tc > 0.62) & (tc < 0.68), 1.0, np.where((tc > 0.76) & (tc < 0.8), 2.0, 0.0))

    if dress in ('coast_f_summer', 'coast_f_rain', 'coast_f_wool'):
        skirt('BARK')
    if dress == 'coast_f_hide':
        cape('HIDE', length=1.55, r1=0.16, tag='dress')
    if dress == 'coast_f_rain' or dress == 'coast_m_rain':
        cape('BARKT', length=1.0, r1=0.2)
    if dress == 'coast_f_wool' or dress == 'coast_m_blanket':
        blanket('WOOL', 1.25 if dress == 'coast_m_blanket' else 1.05, pattern=bands)
    if dress in ('coast_m_fur', 'plateau_m_robe', 'plateau_f_robe'):
        blanket('FUR', 1.15)
    if dress in ('coast_m_summer', 'coast_m_blanket', 'coast_m_fur', 'plateau_m', 'plateau_m_robe', 'coast_m_rain'):
        # breechclout: a small flap front and back
        add(waist + fw * 0.05 * st, waist + fw * 0.07 * st - up * 0.16 * st, 0.045 * st, 0.04 * st,
            'HIDED', caps=False, tag='clout')
    if dress == 'coast_m_hide':
        add(J['chest'] - up * 0.02, J['pelvis'] - up * 0.04, 0.1 * st, 0.12 * st, 'HIDE', caps=False, tag='shirt',
            pattern=lambda tc, xs, ys: (tc > 0.85) * 1.0)
    if dress in ('plateau_f', 'plateau_f_robe'):
        skirt('HIDE', length=0.85, rbot=0.12)
    if dress == 'child_robe':
        blanket('HIDED', 1.1)
    # hats
    hat = spec.get('hat')
    if hat is None:
        hat = 'brim' if dress in ('coast_f_rain', 'coast_m_rain') else ('basket' if dress.startswith('plateau_f')
                                                                         else None)
    if hat == 'brim':
        top = hc + hu * hr * 0.6
        brim_r = 0.16 * st
        # brim: a short, very wide flat capsule seen edge-on, turned to face the camera
        add(top - hu * 0.005 - v3(brim_r, 0, 0) * 0 + v3(0, 0, 0), top + hu * 0.0, brim_r * 0.9, brim_r * 0.9,
            'BARKT', tag='brim', caps=True, clip=None)
        prims[-1].tag = 'brim'
        prims[-1].r0 = prims[-1].r1 = brim_r
        add(top, top + hu * 0.085 * st, 0.08 * st, 0.012 * st, 'BARKT', tag='crown')
    elif hat == 'basket':
        top = hc + hu * hr * 0.35
        add(top, top + hu * 0.06 * st, hr * 1.02, hr * 0.9, 'STRAW', caps=False, tag='bhat',
            pattern=lambda tc, xs, ys: ((tc > 0.4) & (tc < 0.62)) * 1.0)
    # --- props
    prop = spec.get('prop', 'default')
    if prop == 'default':
        prop = default_prop(act)
    hL, hR = J['haL'], J['haR']
    hm = (hL + hR) / 2
    if prop == 'digstick':
        d = nrm(hR - hL) if np.linalg.norm(hR - hL) > 0.02 else nrm(v3(0.3, -1, 0))
        d = nrm(d + v3(0.2, -1.2, 0))
        top = hm - d * 0.1 * st
        bot = top + d * 0.75 * st
        if bot[1] < 0:
            bot = top + d * (top[1] / max(-d[1], 1e-3))
        add(top, bot, 0.012 * st, 0.01 * st, 'WOOD', tag='stick')
        # crossbar on the plateau
        if region == 'plateau':
            add(top - fw * 0.0 + v3(0, 0, 0.05), top + v3(0, 0, -0.05), 0.014 * st, None, 'STONE', tag='antler')
    elif prop == 'basket_back':
        a = J['chest'] - fw * (tw + 0.1 * st) + up * 0.04
        b = J['pelvis'] - fw * (tw + 0.085 * st) + up * 0.03
        add(a, b, 0.105 * st, 0.085 * st, 'STRAW', caps=False, tag='basket',
            pattern=lambda tc, xs, ys: (((ys + xs * 0) % 3) == 0) * 1.0)
        # load showing above the rim
        add(a + up * 0.02, a + up * 0.05, 0.09 * st, 0.06 * st, spec.get('load', 'LEAF'), tag='load')
        # tumpline: basket top to forehead
        fore = hc + hfw * hr * 0.6 + hu * hr * 0.55
        add(a + fw * 0.06, fore, 0.008 * st, None, 'NET', tag='tump')
    elif prop == 'basket_hip':
        c = J['pelvis'] + lat * 0.16 * st - up * 0.08
        add(c + v3(0, -0.06, 0) * st, c + v3(0, 0.05, 0) * st, 0.07 * st, 0.08 * st, 'STRAW', caps=False, tag='basket')
    elif prop == 'firewood':
        c = J['shR'] + up * 0.05
        d = nrm(fw * 0.2 + v3(0, 0.1, 0) + fw)
        add(c - fw * 0.35 * st, c + fw * 0.3 * st, 0.05 * st, 0.045 * st, 'WOOD', tag='wood')
    elif prop == 'maul':
        d = nrm(hm - (J['shL'] + J['shR']) / 2)
        add(hm, hm + d * 0.14 * st, 0.03 * st, 0.042 * st, 'STONE', tag='maul')
    elif prop == 'spear':
        d = nrm(hL - hR) if np.linalg.norm(hL - hR) > 0.03 else nrm(fw - up)
        add(hR - d * 0.35 * st, hR + d * 1.9 * st, 0.011 * st, 0.009 * st, 'WOOD', tag='spear')
    elif prop == 'dipnet':
        d = nrm(hL - hR + v3(0.05, -0.02, 0)) if np.linalg.norm(hL - hR) > 0.03 else nrm(fw - up * 0.7)
        end = hR + d * 2.6 * st
        add(hR - d * 0.3 * st, end, 0.012 * st, 0.01 * st, 'WOOD', tag='pole')
        add(end, end + v3(0, -0.28, 0) * st, 0.13 * st, 0.05 * st, 'NET', prof='spindle', tag='net')
    elif prop == 'paddle':
        d = nrm(hR - hL) if np.linalg.norm(hR - hL) > 0.03 else nrm(-up)
        add(hL - d * 0.08 * st, hR + d * 0.25 * st, 0.011 * st, 0.011 * st, 'WOOD', tag='shaft')
        add(hR + d * 0.2 * st, hR + d * 0.62 * st, 0.012 * st, 0.05 * st, 'WOOD', prof='spindle', tag='blade')
    elif prop == 'pestle':
        add(hm - up * 0.02, hm - up * 0.2 * st, 0.022 * st, 0.03 * st, 'STONE', tag='pestle')
    elif prop == 'staff':
        add(hR + v3(0, 0.08, 0) * st, v3(hR[0] + 0.12 * st, 0.0, hR[2]), 0.012 * st, None, 'WOOD', tag='staff')
    elif prop == 'cradle':
        c = hm + fw * 0.05 * st + up * 0.05
        add(c - v3(0.0, 0.12, 0) * st, c + v3(0.03, 0.14, 0) * st, 0.07 * st, 0.06 * st, 'WOOD', caps=False, tag='cradle')
        add(c + v3(0.04, 0.08, 0) * st, c + v3(0.04, 0.08, 0) * st, 0.045 * st, None, 'SKIN', tag='baby')
        add(c + v3(0.02, -0.02, 0) * st, c + v3(0.02, 0.05, 0) * st, 0.06 * st, None, 'BARKT', tag='wrap')
    elif prop == 'firestick':
        d = nrm(hR - J['elR'])
        add(hR - d * 0.05 * st, hR + d * 0.35 * st, 0.012 * st, None, 'WOOD', tag='stick')
    elif prop == 'bowl':
        add(hm, hm + v3(0, 0.03, 0) * st, 0.05 * st, 0.06 * st, 'WOOD', caps=False, tag='bowl')
    elif prop == 'weaving':
        c = J['pelvis'] + fw * 0.26 * st + up * 0.02
        add(c - lat * 0.1 * st, c + lat * 0.1 * st, 0.05 * st, None, 'STRAW', tag='work')
    Jw = {k: W(v) for k, v in J.items() if not k.startswith('_') and k != 'headup'}
    return prims, Jw, dict(dress=dress, hat=hat, prop=prop, stature=st, facing=facing, region=region)

Write: elements/people/src/render.py
"""Render people into index canvases and composite onto painted grounds.

The shading rule is the atelier's two families: every pixel is lit (n.L > 0) or in shadow, and
the two never mix. Lit: halftone / light / highlight. Shadow: shadow / core. Far-side limbs sit
one step down (at a few pixels tall this is what separates the two legs). A nearer part
crossing a farther part of the same figure leaves a one-step dark seam on the farther one,
which is the "contrast inside the mark" rule at figure scale.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from fig import Canvas, D2R
from person import build, hsh
from pal import M, palette, NSTEP


def light_vec(az=40.0, el=40.0):
    """az: 0 = sun at screen left, 90 = behind the viewer, 180 = right, 270 = behind the subject."""
    a, e = az * D2R, el * D2R
    return np.array([-math.cos(a) * math.cos(e), math.sin(e), math.sin(a) * math.cos(e)])


class Scene:
    """A canvas that many figures and props are drawn into, at a given px per unit."""

    def __init__(self, w, h, scale, elev=10.0, ground_y=None):
        self.cv = Canvas(w, h, elev)
        self.scale = scale
        self.ox = w / 2
        self.oy = ground_y if ground_y is not None else h - 3
        self.figs = []
        self.meta = []

    def add_person(self, spec):
        size_px = self.scale
        prims, J, meta = build(spec, size_px)
        fid = len(self.figs)
        for p in prims:
            self.cv.draw(p, self.ox, self.oy, self.scale, fid)
        self.figs.append(J)
        self.meta.append(dict(meta, spec=spec))
        return J

    def add_prims(self, prims, fid=-2):
        for p in prims:
            self.cv.draw(p, self.ox, self.oy, self.scale, fid)

    def screen(self, P):
        return self.cv.project(np.asarray(P, float), self.ox, self.oy, self.scale)


def shade(cv, L, size_px, fire=None, fire_rgb_w=0.0, seed=0, seam=True, hi=True):
    """Resolve steps into cv.idx. L: sun vector (world). fire: (world pos, strength) optional:
    a point light that lifts the family on faces toward it (night scenes)."""
    idx = cv.idx
    m = idx > 0
    mat = idx // 8
    n = cv.n
    lam = (n * L).sum(-1)
    step = np.where(lam > 0.62, 3, np.where(lam > 0.12, 2 + (lam > 0.3), 1))
    step = np.where(lam > 0.05, np.maximum(step, 2), step)
    if hi and size_px >= 22:
        step = np.where(lam > 0.9, 4, step)
    under = n[..., 1] < -0.55
    step = np.where((lam <= 0.05) & under, 0, step)
    step = step + cv.stepmod
    # patterns
    pat = cv.pat
    H, W = idx.shape
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    colhash = ((xs * 73856093) ^ (seed * 19349663)) % 7
    bark = (mat == M['BARK']) | (mat == M['HIDE']) | (mat == M['BARKT'])
    if size_px >= 14:
        # fringe: alternate strands one step down in the fringe zone
        fr = bark & (pat > 0.5)
        step = np.where(fr & (colhash % 2 == 0), step - 1, step)
    wool = mat == M['WOOL']
    mat = np.where(wool & (pat > 0.5) & (pat < 1.5), M['BAND'], mat)
    mat = np.where(wool & (pat > 1.5), M['DARKB'], mat)
    straw = mat == M['STRAW']
    if size_px >= 16:
        step = np.where(straw & (pat > 0.5), step - 1, step)
    # firelight: warm lift on faces turned to the fire
    if fire is not None:
        fpos, fstr = fire
        # approximate the pixel world position from screen is not stored; use normal toward fire direction
        pass
    # seams: nearer part over a farther part of the same figure
    if seam:
        z = cv.z
        part = cv.part
        fig = cv.fig
        dark = np.zeros_like(m)
        thr = max(0.6, 0.02 * size_px * 1.0)
        for dy, dx in ((-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)):
            zn = np.roll(np.roll(z, dy, 0), dx, 1)
            pn = np.roll(np.roll(part, dy, 0), dx, 1)
            fn = np.roll(np.roll(fig, dy, 0), dx, 1)
            mn = np.roll(np.roll(m, dy, 0), dx, 1)
            dark |= m & mn & (fn == fig) & (pn != part) & (zn > z + thr)
        step = np.where(dark, step - 1, step)
    step = np.clip(step, 0, NSTEP - 1)
    cv.idx = np.where(m, mat * 8 + step, 0).astype(np.int16)
    return cv.idx


def colorize(idx, lut):
    return lut[idx]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# single sprite

def sprite(spec, size_px, elev=10.0, sun=(40, 40), pad=None, hour='noon', seam=True):
    """Render one person alone. Returns (idx, mask, foot_xy)."""
    pad = pad or max(4, int(size_px * 0.9))
    w = int(size_px * 1.6 + 2 * pad)
    h = int(size_px * 1.3 + pad)
    sc = Scene(w, h, size_px, elev, ground_y=h - max(2, pad // 3))
    sc.add_person(spec)
    shade(sc.cv, light_vec(*sun), size_px, seed=spec.get('seed', 0), seam=seam)
    return sc


def crop_to(img, mask, margin=2):
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    if len(ys) == 0:
        return img
    return img[max(0, ys.min() - margin):ys.max() + margin + 1, max(0, xs.min() - margin):xs.max() + margin + 1]

        step = np.where(fr & (colhash % 3 == 0), step - 1, step)

f=f.replace("""def shade(cv,""","""def smooth_normals(cv, it=1):
    \"\"\"Blur normals within each surface group (3x3), so a torso built of several capsules
    shades as one form: clean lit and shadow planes instead of a patchwork.\"\"\"
    n = cv.n.copy()
    g = cv.group
    m = cv.idx > 0
    for _ in range(it):
        acc = n * m[..., None]
        cnt = m.astype(float)
        for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
            for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
                if dy == 0 and dx == 0:
                    continue
                same = m & (np.roll(np.roll(g, dy, 0), dx, 1) == g) & np.roll(np.roll(m, dy, 0), dx, 1)
                acc = acc + np.roll(np.roll(n, dy, 0), dx, 1) * same[..., None]
                cnt = cnt + same
        n = acc / np.maximum(cnt, 1)[..., None]
        n = n / np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(n, axis=-1, keepdims=True), 1e-6)
    return n

Bash: Implement supersampled reduction and compare
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && cat >> render.py <<'EOF'


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# small figures: render at S x, then reduce by salience-weighted mode

SALIENCE = {'HAIR': 1.7, 'WOOD': 2.2, 'STONE': 1.8, 'NET': 2.0, 'STRAW': 1.3, 'BARKT': 1.2, 'WOOL': 1.3,
            'LEAF': 1.2, 'FISH': 1.6, 'SILVER': 1.6, 'BAND': 0.8, 'DARKB': 0.8}


def reduce_idx(idx, S, thresh=0.34, sal=None, thin_mats=('WOOD', 'NET', 'STONE')):
    """idx at S x resolution -> target resolution. A target pixel is filled when enough of its
    block is covered (or it holds a thin prop); its material is the salience-weighted mode; its
    step is the mean step of that material in the block, rounded."""
    sal = sal or SALIENCE
    H, W = idx.shape
    h, w = H // S, W // S
    blk = idx[:h * S, :w * S].reshape(h, S, w, S).transpose(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(h, w, S * S)
    mat = blk // 8
    step = blk % 8
    filled = blk > 0
    cov = filled.mean(-1)
    nm = len(M) + 1
    score = np.zeros((h, w, nm))
    ssum = np.zeros((h, w, nm))
    for mi in range(1, nm):
        sel = (mat == mi) & filled
        if not sel.any():
            continue
        from pal import MATS
        wgt = sal.get(MATS[mi], 1.0) if mi < len(MATS) else 1.0
        score[..., mi] = sel.sum(-1) * wgt
        ssum[..., mi] = (step * sel).sum(-1)
    best = score.argmax(-1)
    cnt = np.take_along_axis(score, best[..., None], -1)[..., 0]
    from pal import MATS
    wbest = np.array([sal.get(MATS[i], 1.0) if 0 < i < len(MATS) else 1.0 for i in range(nm)])[best]
    n_best = cnt / np.maximum(wbest, 1e-6)
    st = np.rint(np.take_along_axis(ssum, best[..., None], -1)[..., 0] / np.maximum(n_best, 1)).astype(int)
    thin = np.isin(best, [M[t] for t in thin_mats]) & (n_best >= max(2, S // 2))
    keep = (cov >= thresh) | thin
    out = np.where(keep, best * 8 + st, 0).astype(np.int16)
    return out


def sprite_ss(spec, size_px, S=4, elev=10.0, sun=(40, 40), pad=None, thresh=0.34):
    """Supersampled sprite for small sizes."""
    pad = pad or max(4, int(size_px * 0.9))
    w = int(size_px * 1.6 + 2 * pad)
    h = int(size_px * 1.3 + pad)
    gy = h - max(2, pad // 3)
    sc = Scene(w * S, h * S, size_px * S, elev, ground_y=gy * S)
    sc.add_person(spec)
    shade(sc.cv, light_vec(*sun), size_px, seed=spec.get('seed', 0), seam=size_px >= 10)
    small = reduce_idx(sc.cv.idx, S, thresh)
    return small, sc
EOF
cat > t4.py <<'EOF'
import numpy as np, sys
from render import sprite, sprite_ss
from pal import palette
from util import up, save, side, vstack
lut = palette('noon')
specs = eval(sys.argv[1])
sizes = [int(x) for x in sys.argv[2].split(',')]

rows=[]

  for mode in ('direct','ss'):
    panels=[]
    for sp in specs:
        sp=dict(sp)
        if mode=='direct':
            idx = sprite(sp, size, pad=4).cv.idx
        else:

        bg=np.array([120,128,112],np.uint8)
        img = np.where(idx[...,None]>0, lut[idx], bg)

save(vstack(rows), '(manager scratchpad)/t4.png')

Bash: Build Bruegel reduction sheet
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && cat > stage0.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 0: what survives of Bruegel's small figures when they are made into pixels.

Bruegel's Harvesters (1565) is the moodboard's lesson in small figures read by task. Ferrari
never painted people in the Living Worlds scenes we have, so this is the closest master for the
subject; Ferrari stays the master for light, palette and mark. For three figures (the mower, the
kneeling binder, the woman bent over a sheaf) the sheet shows: the painting at its native
pixel size, the same quantized to 10 colours, and area-reduced to the heights we need
(about 24, 12 and 6 px) then quantized. Then my figure engine in the same poses and colours.
"""
import numpy as np, sys
from PIL import Image
from util import up, save, side, vstack, grid
sys.path.insert(0, '.')

def kmeans(px, k, it=20, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c = px[rng.choice(len(px), k, replace=False)].astype(float)
    for _ in range(it):
        d = ((px[:, None] - c[None]) ** 2).sum(-1)
        a = d.argmin(1)
        for j in range(k):
            if (a == j).any():
                c[j] = px[a == j].mean(0)
    return c, a

def quant(img, k=10):
    px = img.reshape(-1, 3).astype(float)
    c, a = kmeans(px, k)
    return c[a].reshape(img.shape).astype(np.uint8)

def reduce(img, h):
    im = Image.fromarray(img)
    w = max(1, round(img.shape[1] * h / img.shape[0]))
    return np.asarray(im.resize((w, h), Image.BOX))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    rows = []
    for name, hh in (('scythe', 60), ('kneel', 50), ('bend', 75)):
        b = np.asarray(Image.open(f'../stages/s0/bruegel_{name}.png').convert('RGB'))
        panels = [up(b, 6), up(quant(b), 6)]
        labs = ['Bruegel, native px', 'quantized, 10 colours']
        for f in (0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            r = quant(reduce(b, max(4, int(b.shape[0] * f))), 8)
            k = int(6 / f)
            panels.append(up(r, k))
            labs.append(f'area-reduced x{f}, 8 colours')
        rows.append(side(panels, labs))
    save(vstack(rows), '../stages/s0/s0_bruegel_reduction.png')
EOF

def field(h, w, seed=0):
    """Bruegel's stubble field, as a flat index-free RGB noise of his quantized colours."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cols = np.array([[196, 160, 90], [176, 138, 70], [210, 180, 110], [150, 110, 50]], np.uint8)
    a = rng.choice(4, size=(h, w), p=[0.45, 0.3, 0.15, 0.1])
    return cols[a]


def mine(spec, size, sun=(40, 45)):
    from render import sprite
    from pal import palette
    lut = palette('golden')
    sc = sprite(spec, size, pad=6, sun=sun)
    idx = sc.cv.idx
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(idx > 0)
    idx = idx[ys.min() - 3:ys.max() + 4, xs.min() - 4:xs.max() + 5]
    bg = field(*idx.shape)
    img = np.where(idx[..., None] > 0, lut[idx], bg)
    return img


BR = {'torso': 'WOOL', 'uarm': 'WOOL', 'neck': 'SKIN', 'thigh': 'HIDED', 'shin': 'HIDED', 'hair': 'WOOL',
      'hairlong': 'WOOL'}
POSES = {
    'scythe': dict(activity='spear', sex='m', facing=200, dress='coast_m_summer', hair='short', prop='spear',
                   pose_over=dict(pitch=34, headp=20, hipL=22, hipR=-18, knL=18, knR=10, shR=70, elR=10, shL=40,
                                  elL=10, sabL=40, sabR=20), recolor={**BR, 'hair': 'HIDED', 'clout': 'HIDED'}),
    'kneel': dict(activity='gather', sex='m', facing=190, dress='coast_m_summer', hair='short', prop=None,
                  pose_over=dict(pitch=60, headp=30, shR=60, elR=20, shL=50, elL=30),
                  recolor={**BR, 'torso': 'BAND', 'hair': 'HIDED', 'clout': 'HIDED'}),
    'bend': dict(activity='dig', sex='f', facing=330, dress='coast_f_summer', prop=None,
                 pose_over=dict(pitch=88, headp=30, shL=10, shR=14, elL=10, elR=10, hipL=30, hipR=20, knL=10,
                                knR=6), recolor={**BR, 'skirt': 'FUR', 'hair': 'WOOL'}),
}


def compare():
    rows = []
    for name, hh in (('scythe', 45), ('kneel', 30), ('bend', 55)):
        b = np.asarray(Image.open(f'../stages/s0/bruegel_{name}.png').convert('RGB'))
        q = quant(b)
        panels = [up(q, 6)]
        labs = ['Bruegel quantized (native)']
        for size in (hh, hh // 2, hh // 4):
            panels.append(up(mine(dict(POSES[name]), size), max(3, int(6 * hh / size / 1.0 * (1 if size == hh else 1)))))
            labs.append(f'mine, figure {size} px')
        rows.append(side(panels, labs))
    save(vstack(rows), '../stages/s0/s0_bruegel_vs_mine.png')


if __name__ == '__main__' and len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == 'compare':
    compare()

# output:
