Write: elements/people/src/grounds.py
"""Calm grounds for people to stand on, and the shadows people leave on them.

Stage 0's lesson from Bruegel: a small figure survives only against a calm ground, a field whose
values sit close together, so the figure's own masses (dark hair, a light blanket, a warm skin)
are the loudest thing in its neighbourhood. These grounds are deliberately quieter than the
element students' full painters (cobbles, grass-lod); in a real scene those painters provide the
ground and this module only supplies the contact and cast shadows.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from pal import M


def _rng(seed):
    return np.random.default_rng(seed)


def ground(kind, h, w, scale, seed=0, horizon=0):
    """Index canvas (h, w) of a ground seen at a low angle. scale = px per unit (adult height)."""
    rng = _rng(seed)
    idx = np.zeros((h, w), np.int16)
    mat = M[kind]
    step = np.full((h, w), 2, int)
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    if kind == 'GRAVEL':
        # stones: flattened ellipses, larger near (bottom); lit top row, dark underside
        n = int(h * w / max(2.0, (0.05 * scale) ** 2) * 0.8)
        for _ in range(n):
            y = rng.integers(0, h)
            near = (y + 1) / h
            r = max(0.5, rng.uniform(0.03, 0.09) * scale * (0.6 + 0.6 * near))
            x = rng.integers(0, w)
            ry = max(0.5, r * 0.45)
            y0, y1 = int(max(0, y - ry - 1)), int(min(h, y + ry + 2))
            x0, x1 = int(max(0, x - r - 1)), int(min(w, x + r + 2))
            if y1 <= y0 or x1 <= x0:
                continue
            yy, xx = np.mgrid[y0:y1, x0:x1]
            e = ((xx + 0.5 - x) / r) ** 2 + ((yy + 0.5 - y) / ry) ** 2
            ins = e <= 1
            tone = rng.choice([1, 2, 2, 3], p=[0.2, 0.35, 0.3, 0.15])
            s = np.full(ins.shape, tone)
            if r >= 1.2:
                s = np.where((yy + 0.5 - y) < -ry * 0.35, np.minimum(tone + 1, 3), s)
                s = np.where((yy + 0.5 - y) > ry * 0.45, np.maximum(tone - 1, 1), s)
            step[y0:y1, x0:x1] = np.where(ins, s, step[y0:y1, x0:x1])
    elif kind in ('GRASS', 'STUBBLE'):
        base = rng.random((h, w))
        step = np.where(base < 0.18, 1, np.where(base > 0.86, 3, 2))
        # blades: short vertical clean runs, longer near
        n = int(h * w * 0.08)
        for _ in range(n):
            x, y = rng.integers(0, w), rng.integers(0, h)
            L = 1 + int(rng.random() * (1 + 3 * (y / h)) * max(1, scale / 16))
            s = 3 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 1
            step[max(0, y - L):y, x] = s
    elif kind in ('SAND', 'DIRT', 'MOSS'):
        base = rng.random((h, w))
        step = np.where(base < 0.08, 1, np.where(base > 0.94, 3, 2))
    idx[:] = mat * 8 + step
    return idx


def shadows(scene, L, ground_idx, strength=1, contact=True, cast=True, cast_len=1.0):
    """Darken ground_idx under the figures of a Scene: a contact line where they meet the ground,
    and a cast shadow sheared along the light (the figure mask projected on the ground plane)."""
    cv = scene.cv
    H, W = ground_idx.shape
    out = ground_idx.copy()
    dark = np.zeros((H, W), int)
    e = cv.e
    for fid, J in enumerate(scene.figs):
        m = (cv.fig == fid) & (cv.idx > 0)
        if not m.any():
            continue
        # foot row: ground plane under this figure (y = 0 at its z)
        z0 = (J['anL'][2] + J['anR'][2]) / 2
        foot = scene.oy + z0 * math.sin(e) * scene.scale
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m)
        if cast and L[1] > 0.05:
            hpx = np.maximum(foot - (ys + 0.5), 0) / max(math.cos(e), 0.2)
            dx = -L[0] / L[1] * hpx * cast_len
            dy = (-L[2] / L[1]) * hpx * math.sin(e) * cast_len
            sx = np.rint(xs + dx).astype(int)
            sy = np.rint(foot - 0.5 + dy).astype(int)
            # thicken the sliver a little when seen from low (a shadow on the ground is thin)
            ok = (sx >= 0) & (sx < W) & (sy >= 0) & (sy < H)
            dark[sy[ok], sx[ok]] = np.maximum(dark[sy[ok], sx[ok]], 1)
        if contact:
            # the lowest pixels of the figure that are near the ground: the darkest line under them
            near = ys >= foot - 1.5 - 0.02 * scene.scale
            for x, y in zip(xs[near], ys[near]):
                yy = int(round(foot))
                for ddx in (-1, 0, 1):
                    if 0 <= x + ddx < W and 0 <= yy < H:
                        dark[yy, x + ddx] = max(dark[yy, x + ddx], 2 if ddx == 0 else 1)
    st = out % 8
    mt = out // 8
    st = np.clip(st - dark * strength, 0, 4)
    return np.where(out > 0, mt * 8 + st, 0).astype(np.int16)


def composite(ground_idx, fig_idx):
    return np.where(fig_idx > 0, fig_idx, ground_idx)


def edge_contrast(img_rgb, fig_mask, lab_thresh=0.07, dark_rgb=None):
    """Selective outline: where a figure's edge pixel sits too close in value to the ground beside
    it, darken the figure pixel (on light ground) or lighten it (on dark ground) one notch. Found
    edges where the figure would be lost, lost edges where it already reads."""
    from util import lab_img
    lab = lab_img(img_rgb)
    Lc = lab[..., 0]
    out = img_rgb.copy().astype(float)
    H, W = fig_mask.shape
    for dy, dx in ((1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, 1), (0, -1)):
        nb_m = np.roll(np.roll(fig_mask, dy, 0), dx, 1)
        nb_L = np.roll(np.roll(Lc, dy, 0), dx, 1)
        edge = fig_mask & ~nb_m & (np.abs(Lc - nb_L) < lab_thresh)
        lighter = nb_L < 0.45
        f = np.where(lighter, 1.18, 0.72)
        out[edge] = np.clip(out[edge] * f[edge][:, None], 0, 255)
    return out.astype(np.uint8)
Bash: Scene render with ground and shadows
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && cat >> render.py <<'EOF'


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# whole scene: shade, ground, shadows, edges, palette

def edge_rule(idx, fig_mask, lut, thresh=0.075):
    """Selective outline in index space: a figure pixel on its silhouette whose value is too close
    to the ground beside it moves one step away from the ground's value (darker on light ground,
    lighter on dark). Edges are found only where the figure would otherwise be lost."""
    from util import lab_img
    Ltab = lab_img(lut[None].astype(np.uint8))[0][:, 0]
    L = Ltab[idx]
    st = idx % 8
    mt = idx // 8
    dstep = np.zeros(idx.shape, int)
    for dy, dx in ((1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, 1), (0, -1)):
        nm = np.roll(np.roll(fig_mask, dy, 0), dx, 1)
        nL = np.roll(np.roll(L, dy, 0), dx, 1)
        edge = fig_mask & ~nm & (np.abs(L - nL) < thresh)
        dstep = np.where(edge & (dstep == 0), np.where(nL > L, -1, 1), dstep)
    st = np.clip(st + dstep, 0, NSTEP - 1)
    return np.where(fig_mask, mt * 8 + st, idx).astype(np.int16)


def render_scene(sc, ground_kind='GRAVEL', hour='noon', sun=(40, 40), seed=0, size_px=None, ground_idx=None,
                 edges=True, cast_len=1.0, depth=0.0):
    from grounds import ground, shadows
    L = light_vec(*sun)
    size_px = size_px or sc.scale
    shade(sc.cv, L, size_px, seed=seed)
    H, W = sc.cv.idx.shape
    g = ground_idx if ground_idx is not None else ground(ground_kind, H, W, sc.scale, seed)
    g = shadows(sc, L, g, cast_len=cast_len)
    fm = sc.cv.idx > 0
    idx = np.where(fm, sc.cv.idx, g).astype(np.int16)
    lut = palette(hour, depth)
    if edges:
        idx = edge_rule(idx, fm, lut)
    return lut[idx], idx
EOF

        if ik is not None:
            # two-bone IK: the hand reaches a target given relative to the chest (f, y, l);
            # the elbow bends down, back and out, as elbows do
            T = chest + v3(*ik) * P.get('stature', 1.0)
            u, f_ = P['uarm'], P['farm']
            D = T - sh
            dist = np.clip(np.linalg.norm(D), abs(u - f_) + 1e-3, u + f_ - 1e-3)
            Dn = nrm(D)
            a_ = (u * u - f_ * f_ + dist * dist) / (2 * dist)
            h_ = math.sqrt(max(u * u - a_ * a_, 0))
            pole = -up * 1.0 - tf * 0.25 + tl * sg * 0.55
            pole = nrm(pole - Dn * (pole @ Dn))

            J['ha' + s] = sh + Dn * dist"""

s=open('person.py').read()
def reps(start, end, new):
    global s
    a=s.index(start); b=s.index(end, a)
    s=s[:a]+new+s[b:]
reps("    if prop == 'digstick':","    elif prop == 'basket_back':","""    if prop == 'digstick':
        # a fire-hardened digging stick through both hands, down to the ground in front
        d = nrm(hL - hR) if np.linalg.norm(hL - hR) > 0.02 else nrm(v3(0.3, -1, 0))
        top = hR - d * 0.08 * st
        t_ground = (top[1]) / max(-d[1], 1e-3)
        bot = top + d * min(t_ground, 0.8 * st)
        add(top, bot, 0.013 * st, 0.011 * st, 'WOOD', tag='stick')
        if region == 'plateau':      # antler cross-handle
            add(top + v3(0, 0, 0.05 * st), top - v3(0, 0, 0.05 * st), 0.014 * st, None, 'STONE', tag='antler')
""")
reps("    elif prop == 'spear':","    elif prop == 'dipnet':","""    elif prop == 'spear':
        d = nrm(hL - hR)
        add(hR - d * 0.4 * st, hR + d * 1.8 * st, 0.011 * st, 0.009 * st, 'WOOD', tag='spear')
""")
reps("    elif prop == 'dipnet':","    elif prop == 'paddle':","""    elif prop == 'dipnet':

""")

Write: elements/people/src/setting.py
"""Things people work with and around: a hearth and its fire, a dugout canoe, a salmon weir, a
drying rack, mats, baskets on the ground. Capsule props go through the same z-buffer as the
figures; fire and water are painted in index space after shading.

Sizes from the naturalist's guides 03/04/06: canoes 3-9 m (Twana local canoes rarely above
6.7 x 1.2 m), paddles 1.2-1.5 m, weirs of stakes and lattice panels across the river with a
platform for the dip-netters, drying racks of poles with split salmon.
Units are adult heights (~1.65 m), so 1 m ~ 0.6 units.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from fig import Prim, v3, D2R
from pal import M

MU = 1 / 1.65          # one metre in units


def hearth(x=0.0, z=0.0, scale=1.0, n_logs=4, seed=0):
    """A ring of stones and a few burning sticks meeting in the middle."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    P = []
    R = 0.32 * MU / 0.6 * scale * 0.6
    for i in range(9):
        a = i / 9 * 2 * math.pi + rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2)
        c = v3(x + math.cos(a) * R, 0.035, z + math.sin(a) * R * 0.9)
        P.append(Prim(c, c, 0.045 * rng.uniform(0.8, 1.2), None, 'STONE', tag='hstone', minw=0.4))
    for i in range(n_logs):
        a = i / n_logs * 2 * math.pi + rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3)
        o = v3(x + math.cos(a) * R * 0.9, 0.03, z + math.sin(a) * R * 0.8)
        c = v3(x, 0.07, z)
        P.append(Prim(o, c, 0.022, 0.016, 'LOG', tag='hlog', minw=0.4))
    return P


def canoe(x=0.0, z=0.0, length=3.6, beam=0.42, depth=0.22, heading=0.0, waterline=0.06, kind='salish'):
    """A dugout hull: a long spindle cut off at the gunwale, the inside darker. heading in degrees
    (0 = pointing screen right). Returns prims; the hull's upper half is clipped by the caller's
    gunwale test (we use two prims: the outer hull below the gunwale, the dark interior)."""
    h = heading * D2R
    ax = v3(math.cos(h), 0, math.sin(h))
    c = v3(x, waterline + depth * 0.1, z)
    a, b = c - ax * length / 2, c + ax * length / 2
    P = []
    # outer hull: a spindle, only the part below the gunwale plane is kept (clip set in Scene)
    hull = Prim(a, b, beam * 0.1, beam * 0.5, 'WOOD', prof='spindle', tag='hull', minw=0.5)
    hull.gunwale = c[1]
    P.append(hull)
    # the dark interior seen from above: a slightly narrower flat spindle at the gunwale
    inner = Prim(a + ax * 0.12, b - ax * 0.12, beam * 0.05, beam * 0.42, 'DARKB', prof='spindle', tag='hullin',
                 minw=0.4)
    inner.flat = c[1]
    P.append(inner)
    # the bow: a raised prow for the Salish canoe
    if kind == 'salish':
        P.append(Prim(b - ax * 0.25 + v3(0, depth * 0.3, 0), b + ax * 0.12 + v3(0, depth * 0.9, 0), 0.03, 0.02,
                      'WOOD', tag='prow'))
    return P


def weir(x0=-2.0, x1=2.0, z=0.0, n=None, h=0.9, water=0.25):
    """A line of stakes across the river with lattice panels between, and a stringer on top."""
    P = []
    n = n or int((x1 - x0) / 0.18) + 1
    for i in range(n):
        x = x0 + (x1 - x0) * i / (n - 1)
        lean = 0.06 * math.sin(i * 1.7)
        P.append(Prim(v3(x, -0.1, z), v3(x + lean, h + 0.05 * math.sin(i * 2.3), z - 0.05), 0.022, 0.018, 'WOOD',
                      tag='stake', minw=0.4))
    for yy in (water + 0.08, water + 0.25, h - 0.08):
        P.append(Prim(v3(x0, yy, z + 0.02), v3(x1, yy, z + 0.02), 0.012, None, 'NET', tag='lattice'))
    return P


def platform(x, z, w=0.9, h=0.55):
    """A small fishing platform of poles on the weir."""
    P = []
    for dx in (-w / 2, w / 2):
        for dz in (-0.15, 0.15):
            P.append(Prim(v3(x + dx, -0.1, z + dz), v3(x + dx, h, z + dz), 0.02, None, 'WOOD', tag='ppost', minw=0.4))
    P.append(Prim(v3(x - w / 2 - 0.05, h, z), v3(x + w / 2 + 0.05, h, z), 0.045, None, 'LOG', tag='pdeck',
                  minw=0.5))
    return P


def rack(x=0.0, z=0.0, w=1.6, h=1.05, fish=6, seed=0):
    """A drying rack: two forked posts, a crossbar, split salmon hanging red."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    P = [Prim(v3(x - w / 2, 0, z), v3(x - w / 2, h, z), 0.022, None, 'WOOD', tag='rpost', minw=0.4),
         Prim(v3(x + w / 2, 0, z), v3(x + w / 2, h, z), 0.022, None, 'WOOD', tag='rpost', minw=0.4),
         Prim(v3(x - w / 2 - 0.08, h - 0.02, z), v3(x + w / 2 + 0.08, h - 0.02, z), 0.016, None, 'WOOD', tag='rbar')]
    for i in range(fish):
        fx = x - w / 2 + w * (i + 0.5) / fish + rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03)
        P.append(Prim(v3(fx, h - 0.04, z), v3(fx, h - 0.34 - rng.uniform(0, 0.06), z), 0.045, 0.03, 'FISH',
                      prof='spindle', tag='rfish', minw=0.5))
    return P


def ground_basket(x, z, r=0.1, h=0.16, mat='STRAW'):
    return [Prim(v3(x, 0.0, z), v3(x, h, z), r, r * 1.1, mat, caps=False, tag='gbasket', minw=0.5)]


def mat_on_ground(x, z, w=0.9, d=0.6):
    return [Prim(v3(x - w / 2, 0.01, z), v3(x + w / 2, 0.01, z), d / 2, None, 'MAT', tag='gmat', minw=0.4)]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# fire and smoke, painted into the index canvas

def paint_fire(idx, cx, cy, size_px, phase=0.0, seed=0, zbuf=None):
    """A small cooking fire at screen (cx, cy) (its base), for a figure scale of size_px px.
    Embers along the base, two or three flame tongues whose tips flicker with phase, a
    bright core. Flame height ~0.3 units."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    H, W = idx.shape
    fh = max(2.0, 0.34 * size_px)
    fw = max(1.5, 0.22 * size_px)
    out = idx.copy()
    nt = 3 if size_px >= 14 else 2
    for t in range(nt):
        off = (t - (nt - 1) / 2) * fw * 0.55
        hh = fh * (0.6 + 0.4 * abs(math.sin(phase * 2 * math.pi * (1 + 0.37 * t) + t * 1.3)))
        ww = fw * 0.45
        for y in range(int(math.ceil(hh)) + 1):
            frac = y / max(hh, 1)
            half = ww * (1 - frac) ** 0.8
            sway = math.sin(phase * 6.28 * 1.3 + y * 0.7 + t) * 0.35 * frac * fw * 0.3
            x0 = int(round(cx + off + sway - half))
            x1 = int(round(cx + off + sway + half))
            yy = int(round(cy - y))
            if not (0 <= yy < H):
                continue
            for xx in range(max(0, x0), min(W, x1 + 1)):
                core = abs(xx + 0.5 - (cx + off + sway)) < half * 0.45 and frac < 0.55
                st = 4 if core else (3 if frac < 0.75 else 2)
                if frac > 0.85:
                    st = 1
                out[yy, xx] = M['FIRE'] * 8 + st
    # embers
    for xx in range(int(cx - fw * 0.6), int(cx + fw * 0.6) + 1):
        if 0 <= xx < W and 0 <= int(cy) < H:
            out[int(cy), xx] = M['FIRE'] * 8 + (2 if rng.random() < 0.6 else 1)
    return out


def paint_smoke(idx, cx, cy, size_px, phase=0.0, seed=0, drift=0.6, rows=None, hour_dark=False):
    """Blue woodsmoke rising and leaning with the wind: a dithered column that thins upward."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    H, W = idx.shape
    out = idx.copy()
    L = int(rows or size_px * 1.8)
    for y in range(L):
        frac = y / L
        xc = cx + drift * y * (0.3 + frac) + math.sin(frac * 7 + phase * 6.28) * size_px * 0.05
        half = max(0.5, size_px * (0.05 + 0.18 * frac))
        for xx in range(int(xc - half), int(xc + half) + 1):
            yy = int(cy - y)
            if not (0 <= yy < H and 0 <= xx < W):
                continue
            p = 0.75 * (1 - frac) ** 1.2
            if ((xx + yy) % 2 == 0 and rng.random() < p * 1.3) or rng.random() < p * 0.35:
                out[yy, xx] = M['SMOKE'] * 8 + (2 if frac < 0.4 else 1 if hour_dark else 2)
    return out

    cv = sc.cv
    H, W = cv.idx.shape
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    ce, se = math.cos(cv.e), math.sin(cv.e)
    su = (sc.oy - (ys + 0.5)) / sc.scale
    dz = cv.z / sc.scale
    x = (xs + 0.5 - sc.ox) / sc.scale
    y = su * ce + dz * se
    z = -su * se + dz * ce
    return np.stack([x, y, z], -1)


def fire_ground(sc, g, fires, size_px, seed=0):
    \"\"\"Lift the ground's steps around each fire (radial, dithered at the edge).\"\"\"
    H, W = g.shape
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    ce, se = math.cos(sc.cv.e), math.sin(sc.cv.e)
    # ground-plane coordinates of each pixel (y = 0)
    su = (sc.oy - (ys + 0.5)) / sc.scale
    zw = -su / max(se, 1e-3)
    xw = (xs + 0.5 - sc.ox) / sc.scale
    lift = np.zeros((H, W))
    for fp, reach in fires:
        d = np.hypot(xw - fp[0], zw - fp[2])
        lift = np.maximum(lift, 2.6 / (1 + (d / (reach * 0.8)) ** 2))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    lift = np.floor(lift + rng.random((H, W)) * 0.999 * ((ys + xs) % 2 * 0.5 + 0.25)).astype(int)
    st = np.clip(g % 8 + lift - 1, 0, 4)
    return np.where(g > 0, (g // 8) * 8 + st, 0).astype(np.int16)


def render_scene(sc, ground_kind='GRAVEL', hour='noon', sun=(40, 40), seed=0, size_px=None, ground_idx=None,
                 edges=True, cast_len=1.0, depth=0.0, fires=None, post=None):
    \"\"\"fires: list of ((x, y, z), reach) world positions of firelight; post: callback(idx, sc) for
    painting fire/smoke/water in index space before the palette.\"\"\"
    from grounds import ground, shadows
    L = light_vec(*sun)
    size_px = size_px or sc.scale
    night = hour == 'night'
    wp = world_pos(sc) if fires else None
    shade(sc.cv, L, size_px, seed=seed, fire=fires, wpos=wp, sun_on=not night)
    H, W = sc.cv.idx.shape
    g = ground_idx if ground_idx is not None else ground(ground_kind, H, W, sc.scale, seed)
    if night:
        g = np.where(g > 0, (g // 8) * 8 + np.clip(g % 8 - 1, 0, 4), 0).astype(np.int16)
        if fires:
            g = fire_ground(sc, g, fires, size_px, seed)
    else:
        g = shadows(sc, L, g, cast_len=cast_len)"""
assert old in r

    return lut[idx], idx"""

        idx = edge_rule(idx, fm, lut)
    if post is not None:
        idx = post(idx, sc)
    return lut[idx], idx"""
r=r.replace(old,new)
open('render.py','w').write(r)

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np, math
from render import Scene, render_scene
from setting import hearth, paint_fire, paint_smoke
from util import up, save, side
hour = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else 'night'

for size in (32,16,8):
    sc=Scene(int(size*3.4), int(size*1.9), size, 14, ground_y=int(size*1.55))
    sc.add_prims(hearth(0,0))
    ring=[(-0.55,0.1,20,'sit_log','f','coast_f_wool'),(0.55,0.05,160,'sit','m','coast_m_fur'),(0.0,-0.55,90,'fire','f','coast_f_summer'),(-0.35,0.55,300,'sit','m','coast_m_blanket'),(0.45,-0.45,120,'weave','f','coast_f_summer')]

        return idx
    rgb,_=render_scene(sc,'DIRT',hour,(40,40),fires=fires,post=post)
    rows.append(up(rgb,{32:5,16:10,8:20}[size]))

s=open('setting.py').read()

s=s[:a]+'''def paint_fire(idx, cx, cy, size_px, phase=0.0, seed=0, zbuf=None, zfire=None):
    """A small cooking fire whose base is at screen (cx, cy), for a figure scale of size_px px.
    Two or three teardrop tongues (each a hot core inside a cooler rim), tips flickering with
    phase; embers along the base. Flame height ~0.3 units. Pixels of the scene nearer than the
    fire (zbuf > zfire) are left alone, so people in front of the hearth hide it."""
    H, W = idx.shape
    out = idx.copy()
    fh = max(2.2, 0.3 * size_px)
    fw = max(1.6, 0.2 * size_px)
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    px, py = xs + 0.5, ys + 0.5
    heat = np.zeros((H, W))
    nt = 3 if size_px >= 14 else 2
    for t in range(nt):
        off = (t - (nt - 1) / 2) * fw * 0.42
        hh = fh * (0.55 + 0.45 * abs(math.sin(phase * 2 * math.pi * (1 + 0.37 * t) + t * 1.9)))
        if t == (nt - 1) // 2:
            hh = max(hh, fh * 0.85)
        v = np.clip((cy - py) / hh, 0, 1.2)                      # 0 at the base, 1 at the tip
        sway = np.sin(phase * 6.28 + t * 2.1) * 0.25 * fw * v ** 2
        half = fw * 0.34 * np.sqrt(np.maximum(0, 1 - v)) * (1 + 0.6 * np.exp(-((v - 0.25) / 0.25) ** 2))
        d = np.abs(px - (cx + off + sway)) / np.maximum(half, 1e-3)
        inside = (d <= 1) & (py <= cy + 0.5) & (v < 1)
        heat = np.maximum(heat, np.where(inside, (1 - d) * 0.6 + (1 - v) * 0.6, 0))
    st = np.where(heat > 0.85, 4, np.where(heat > 0.45, 3, np.where(heat > 0.15, 2, 1)))
    m = heat > 0
    # embers along the base
    ex = (np.abs(px - cx) <= fw * 0.6) & (np.abs(py - cy) < 0.6)
    st = np.where(ex & ~m, 2, st)
    m = m | ex
    if zbuf is not None and zfire is not None:
        m &= ~(zbuf > zfire)
    out[m] = M['FIRE'] * 8 + st[m]
    return out

Write: elements/people/src/water.py
"""Water for people who stand in it, fish from it and paddle on it.

The studio's measured rules (master copy, analysis, willow stage 5): the reflection is an exact
mirror with a compressed range (lights drop, darks stay), cut by horizontal dash rows, and the
water's own colour carries a sheen band under reflected objects. Here the mirror is exact because
the z-buffer gives every pixel a world position: each object pixel is reflected about the water
plane y = wl and re-projected. Parts below the plane are hidden (legs in the river, stake feet).
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from pal import M


def water_ground(h, w, scale, seed=0, calm=0.7):
    """Water index canvas: base step 2, dark and light dashes in horizontal rows, longer near."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step = np.full((h, w), 2, int)
    for y in range(h):
        near = (y + 1) / h
        n = int(w * (1 - calm) * 0.25 * (0.5 + near))
        for _ in range(n):
            L = max(1, int(rng.integers(1, 3 + int(near * scale * 0.12))))
            x = int(rng.integers(0, w))
            step[y, x:x + L] = 3 if rng.random() < 0.45 else 1
    return (M['WATER'] * 8 + step).astype(np.int16)


def reflect(sc, idx_fig, wpos, water_mask, wl=0.0, seed=0, drop=1, dash=0.22):
    """Return (idx of reflections, mask). idx_fig: shaded figure/prop index canvas."""
    H, W = idx_fig.shape
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = np.zeros((H, W), np.int16)
    m = idx_fig > 0
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(m)
    P = wpos[ys, xs]
    above = P[:, 1] >= wl
    ys, xs, P = ys[above], xs[above], P[above]
    ce, se = math.cos(sc.cv.e), math.sin(sc.cv.e)
    yr = 2 * wl - P[:, 1]
    su = yr * ce - P[:, 2] * se
    ry = np.rint(sc.oy - su * sc.scale - 0.5).astype(int)
    ok = (ry >= 0) & (ry < H)
    ys, xs, ry = ys[ok], xs[ok], ry[ok]
    src = idx_fig[ys, xs]
    st = np.clip(src % 8 - drop, 0, 4)
    # lights drop more than darks (compressed range)
    st = np.where(src % 8 >= 3, st - 1, st)
    st = np.clip(st, 0, 4)
    val = (src // 8) * 8 + st
    # dash rows: some rows of the reflection break up
    rowbreak = rng.random(H) < dash
    keep = ~rowbreak[ry] | (rng.random(len(ry)) < 0.35)
    ys, xs, ry, val = ys[keep], xs[keep], ry[keep], val[keep]
    ok = water_mask[ry, xs]
    out[ry[ok], xs[ok]] = val[ok]
    return out, out > 0


def submerge(idx, wpos, fig_mask, wl, water_idx):
    """Hide the parts of figures and props below the water plane."""
    under = fig_mask & (wpos[..., 1] < wl - 0.005)
    return np.where(under, water_idx, idx), under

Bash: Stage 1 build-up images
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && cat > stage1.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 1: one figure, built up one layer at a time (32 px, shown at 8x).

skeleton -> capsule masses (flat) -> dress -> two light families (greyscale) -> colour ->
on the ground with contact shadow, cast shadow and the edge rule. Then the notan and the colour
figure from four sides.
"""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np
from render import Scene, render_scene, shade, light_vec, sprite
from fig import bresenham
from pal import palette, grey_lut, M
from util import up, save, side, vstack, to_lab, to_rgb

OUT = '../stages/s1/'
import os; os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
BG = np.array([112, 120, 108], np.uint8)
SIZE = 32
K = 8


def mk(spec, size=SIZE, w=None, h=None):
    w = w or int(size * 1.3)
    h = h or int(size * 1.25)
    sc = Scene(w, h, size, 10, ground_y=h - 3)
    sc.add_person(spec)
    return sc


def skeleton_img(spec):
    sc = mk(spec)
    J = sc.figs[0]
    img = np.full(sc.cv.idx.shape + (3,), BG, np.uint8)
    bones = [('pelvis', 'mid'), ('mid', 'chest'), ('chest', 'neck'), ('neck', 'head'), ('chest', 'shL'),
             ('chest', 'shR'), ('shL', 'elL'), ('elL', 'haL'), ('shR', 'elR'), ('elR', 'haR'), ('pelvis', 'hpL'),
             ('pelvis', 'hpR'), ('hpL', 'knL'), ('knL', 'anL'), ('anL', 'toL'), ('hpR', 'knR'), ('knR', 'anR'),
             ('anR', 'toR')]
    for a, b in bones:
        A, B = sc.screen(J[a]), sc.screen(J[b])
        col = (240, 230, 200) if a[-1] != 'L' and b[-1] != 'L' else (150, 140, 120)
        for x, y in bresenham(A[0], A[1], B[0], B[1]):
            if 0 <= y < img.shape[0] and 0 <= x < img.shape[1]:
                img[y, x] = col
    return img


FLAT = {M['SKIN']: (170, 110, 80), M['HAIR']: (20, 18, 24), M['BARK']: (110, 70, 44), M['NET']: (100, 90, 70)}


def flat_img(spec):
    sc = mk(spec)
    idx = sc.cv.idx
    img = np.full(idx.shape + (3,), BG, np.uint8)
    for m, c in FLAT.items():
        img[idx // 8 == m] = c
    other = (idx > 0) & ~np.isin(idx // 8, list(FLAT))
    img[other] = (150, 150, 150)
    return img


def shaded(spec, lut, sun=(40, 40)):
    sc = mk(spec)
    shade(sc.cv, light_vec(*sun), SIZE)
    idx = sc.cv.idx
    return np.where(idx[..., None] > 0, lut[idx], BG)


def notan(spec, sun=(40, 40)):
    sc = mk(spec)
    shade(sc.cv, light_vec(*sun), SIZE, seam=False)
    idx = sc.cv.idx
    st = idx % 8
    img = np.full(idx.shape + (3,), (128, 128, 128), np.uint8)
    img[(idx > 0) & (st >= 2)] = (235, 235, 235)
    img[(idx > 0) & (st < 2)] = (30, 30, 30)
    return img


def grounded(spec, hour='noon'):
    sc = mk(spec, w=int(SIZE * 1.6))
    rgb, _ = render_scene(sc, 'SAND', hour, (40, 40))
    return rgb


if __name__ == '__main__':
    lut = palette('noon')
    g = grey_lut()
    base = dict(activity='stand', facing=20, sex='f', dress='coast_f_summer', phase=0.2)
    naked = dict(base, dress='child_bare', hair='loose')
    panels = [skeleton_img(base), flat_img(naked), flat_img(base), shaded(base, g), shaded(base, lut), grounded(base)]
    labs = ['1 skeleton', '2 capsule masses', '3 dress (bark skirt)', '4 two families, grey', '5 colour',
            '6 ground, shadows, edges']
    save(side([up(p, K) for p in panels], labs), OUT + 's1_buildup.png')
    rows = []
    for f in (0, 60, 120, 200, 270):
        sp = dict(base, facing=f)
        rows.append([notan(sp), shaded(sp, g), shaded(sp, lut)])
    cells = [up(p, 6) for r in rows for p in r]
    from util import grid
    save(grid(cells, 3, [f'facing {f}: {k}' for f in (0, 60, 120, 200, 270) for k in ('notan', 'grey', 'colour')],
              fs=12), OUT + 's1_facings.png')
    # the same for a man in a blanket and a walking woman
    for name, sp in (('man_blanket', dict(activity='stand', sex='m', dress='coast_m_blanket', facing=30)),
                     ('walk', dict(activity='walk', sex='f', phase=0.1, facing=0))):
        panels = [notan(sp), shaded(sp, g), shaded(sp, lut), grounded(sp)]
        save(side([up(p, K) for p in panels], ['notan', 'grey', 'colour', 'grounded']), OUT + f's1_{name}.png')
EOF

"""Stage 4: the size ladder, 32 px down to 6 px, for the figures that matter most."""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np, os
from render import Scene, render_scene
from util import up, save, grid, side, vstack
OUT = '../stages/s4/'; os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
SIZES = (32, 24, 18, 14, 11, 8, 6)
SPECS = [
    ('woman walking', dict(activity='walk', phase=0.1, facing=15)),
    ('carrying a basket', dict(activity='carry', phase=0.3, facing=15)),
    ('rain cape and hat', dict(activity='walk', phase=0.6, facing=160, dress='coast_f_rain')),
    ('digging camas', dict(activity='dig', phase=0.5, facing=20, region='plateau', dress='plateau_f')),
    ('man in a blanket', dict(activity='stand', facing=100, sex='m', dress='coast_m_blanket')),
    ('spearing fish', dict(activity='spear', phase=0.5, facing=10, sex='m')),
    ('splitting cedar', dict(activity='split', phase=0.1, facing=0, sex='m')),
    ('at the fire', dict(activity='fire', phase=0.5, facing=20)),
    ('child running', dict(activity='run', phase=0.2, facing=0, age='child')),
]


def ladder(spec, kind='SAND', hour='noon', k=6):
    cells = []
    for s in SIZES:
        W, H = int(s * 1.7), int(s * 1.35)
        sc = Scene(W, H, s, 10, ground_y=H - max(2, s // 8))
        sc.add_person(dict(spec, seed=4))
        rgb, _ = render_scene(sc, kind, hour, (40, 40))
        cells.append(up(rgb, k))
    return cells


if __name__ == '__main__':
    rows = []
    for name, sp in SPECS:
        cells = ladder(sp)
        rows.append(side(cells, [f'{name} {s}px' for s in SIZES], fs=11))
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 's4_ladder_6x.png')
    # true size, 1x and 2x, to judge how they read at real scale
    rows = []
    for name, sp in SPECS:
        rows.append(side(ladder(sp, k=2), None))
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 's4_ladder_2x.png')

"""Group compositions: people at work, at rest and at play, at several distances.

Each scene is a function scene_x(size_px, elev, hour, phase, seed) -> (rgb, idx). size_px is the
height in px of a standing adult at the scene's reference depth (z = 0). The canvas is laid out in
world units so that the same scene can be rendered at 32, 16, 8 or 5 px per person and at the
low landscape view (10-14 deg) or the iso view (30 deg).

Backdrops are deliberately plain (a far shore, a forest wall): they belong to the other element
students; here they only give the figures a value to read against.
"""
import math
import numpy as np
from render import Scene, render_scene, world_pos
from setting import hearth, paint_fire, paint_smoke, canoe, weir, platform, rack, ground_basket, mat_on_ground
from pal import M
from grounds import ground

rngs = np.random.default_rng


def _scene(size_px, wu, hu, elev, gy_u):
    W, H = int(round(size_px * wu)), int(round(size_px * hu))
    return Scene(W, H, size_px, elev, ground_y=int(round(size_px * gy_u)))


def backdrop(idx, sc, z_far, mat='FOREST', crest_seed=0, amp=0.35):
    """Paint rows beyond depth z_far (screen rows above the ground line of z_far) as a flat far
    band (a forest wall or far shore), its top edge a fringe of conifer spires."""
    H, W = idx.shape
    rng = rngs(crest_seed)
    row = int(round(sc.oy + z_far * math.sin(sc.cv.e) * sc.scale))
    top = row - int(round((0.9 + amp) * sc.scale * 0.8))
    out = idx.copy()
    heights = np.zeros(W)
    x = 0
    while x < W:
        w = int(rng.integers(max(2, sc.scale // 6), max(3, sc.scale // 3) + 1))
        hgt = rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0) * sc.scale * 0.8
        for i in range(w):
            if x + i < W:
                t = abs(i - w / 2) / (w / 2)
                heights[x + i] = hgt * (1 - t * 0.85)
        x += w
    for xx in range(W):
        y0 = int(row - sc.scale * 0.5 - heights[xx])
        for yy in range(max(0, y0), max(0, min(H, row))):
            if out[yy, xx] == 0 or True:
                st = 1 if (xx + yy) % 5 else 2
                out[yy, xx] = M[mat] * 8 + st
    return out, row


def _mix_idx(fig_idx, bg_idx):
    return np.where(fig_idx > 0, fig_idx, bg_idx)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def scene_fire(size_px=24, elev=14, hour='night', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """Around the hearth at night: an old man telling, women weaving and tending, children, a
    baby on a cradleboard."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 4.6, 2.3, elev, 1.75)
    sc.add_prims(hearth(0, 0, seed=seed))
    people = [(-0.95, 0.05, 5, dict(activity='sit_log', sex='f', dress='coast_f_wool')),
              (0.95, -0.05, 175, dict(activity='sit', sex='m', age='elder', dress='coast_m_fur')),
              (0.1, -0.85, 95, dict(activity='fire', sex='f')),
              (-0.5, 0.85, 290, dict(activity='sit', sex='m', dress='coast_m_blanket')),
              (0.7, -0.75, 125, dict(activity='weave', sex='f')),
              (0.55, 0.8, 240, dict(activity='squat_play', age='child', sex='m')),
              (-0.85, -0.7, 60, dict(activity='hold_baby', sex='f', dress='coast_f_wool')),
              (1.6, 0.4, 200, dict(activity='sit_eat', sex='m', dress='coast_m_summer'))]
    for x, z, f, sp in people:
        sc.add_person(dict(sp, x=x, z=z, facing=f, phase=phase, seed=seed + int(x * 7 + z * 3)))
    fires = [((0, 0.12, 0), 0.6)] if hour in ('night', 'dusk') else None

    def post(idx, sc):
        p = sc.screen((0, 0, 0))
        idx = paint_fire(idx, p[0], p[1] - 0.5, size_px, phase=phase, zbuf=sc.cv.z, zfire=p[2])
        idx = paint_smoke(idx, p[0], p[1] - 0.45 * size_px, size_px, phase=phase, hour_dark=hour == 'night',
                          rows=int(size_px * 1.2))
        return idx
    return render_scene(sc, 'DIRT', hour, (40, 35), seed=seed, fires=fires, post=post)


def scene_weir(size_px=24, elev=14, hour='overcast', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """Autumn at the weir: dip-netters on the platforms, a spear fisher in the shallows, a canoe,
    women carrying fish up the gravel bar to the racks."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 7.0, 2.8, elev, 2.3)
    wz = -1.6
    sc.add_prims(weir(-3.6, 3.6, wz, n=30, h=1.0, water=0.3, size_px=size_px))
    sc.add_prims(platform(-1.2, wz + 0.15, w=0.8, h=0.62))
    sc.add_prims(platform(1.4, wz + 0.15, w=0.8, h=0.62))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='dipnet', x=-1.25, z=wz + 0.15, facing=70, sex='m', phase=phase + 0.2))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='dipnet', x=1.35, z=wz + 0.15, facing=110, sex='m', phase=phase + 0.6,
                       dress='coast_m_summer'))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='spear', x=-2.4, z=-0.4, facing=30, sex='m', phase=phase))
    sc.add_prims(canoe(2.3, -0.5, length=2.8, heading=190, waterline=0.3))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='paddle', x=2.25, z=-0.5, facing=190, sex='m', phase=phase + 0.3))
    sc.add_prims(rack(-0.4, 1.35, w=1.3, h=1.0, fish=6, seed=seed))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='carry_load', x=0.8, z=0.9, facing=160, sex='f', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='carry', x=2.0, z=1.1, facing=185, sex='f', phase=phase + 0.5,
                       load='FISH'))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='stand', x=-1.2, z=1.25, facing=60, sex='f', dress='coast_f_wool', phase=phase))

    def post(idx, sc):
        out, _ = backdrop(idx, sc, -3.2, 'FOREST', seed)
        return np.where(sc.cv.idx > 0, idx, np.where(out != idx, out, idx))
    return render_scene(sc, 'GRAVEL', hour, (60, 35), seed=seed, water=dict(wl=0.3, z_shore=0.55, calm=0.55),
                        post=post)


def scene_carry(size_px=24, elev=12, hour='morning', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """A file of people going along the beach: women with loaded baskets on tumplines, a man with
    salmon, a woman in a rain cape, a child running ahead, an elder with a staff."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 6.5, 2.2, elev, 1.75)
    file_ = [(-2.6, 0.2, dict(activity='staff', sex='m', age='elder', dress='coast_m_fur')),
             (-1.8, 0.1, dict(activity='carry', sex='f', load='ROOT')),
             (-1.0, 0.25, dict(activity='carry', sex='f', dress='coast_f_rain', load='LEAF')),
             (-0.2, 0.05, dict(activity='carry_load', sex='m')),
             (0.6, 0.2, dict(activity='walk', sex='f', dress='coast_f_wool')),
             (1.3, 0.1, dict(activity='carry', sex='f', load='FISH')),
             (2.3, 0.3, dict(activity='run', age='child', sex='m')),
             (2.8, 0.0, dict(activity='run', age='child', sex='f'))]
    for i, (x, z, sp) in enumerate(file_):
        sc.add_person(dict(sp, x=x, z=z, facing=0 if sp['activity'] != 'staff' else 5,
                           phase=(phase + i * 0.29) % 1, seed=seed + i))

    def post(idx, sc):
        out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -1.4, 'WATER', seed, amp=-0.9)
        return np.where(sc.cv.idx > 0, idx, np.where(out != idx, out, idx))
    return render_scene(sc, 'GRAVEL', hour, (30, 30), seed=seed, post=post)


def scene_camas(size_px=24, elev=14, hour='morning', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """May on the prairie: women digging camas with sticks, baskets beside them, a child playing,
    a woman carrying a full basket away."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 6.5, 2.6, elev, 2.15)
    rng = rngs(seed)
    diggers = [(-2.4, -0.8, 20), (-1.2, -0.3, 200), (0.1, -1.0, 340), (1.2, -0.2, 160), (2.3, -0.9, 30),
               (-0.6, 0.6, 10)]
    for i, (x, z, f) in enumerate(diggers):
        act = 'dig' if i % 3 != 2 else 'gather'
        sc.add_person(dict(activity=act, x=x, z=z, facing=f, sex='f', region='coast',
                           phase=(phase + i * 0.37) % 1, seed=seed + i))
        sc.add_prims(ground_basket(x + (0.3 if math.cos(math.radians(f)) < 0 else -0.3), z + 0.15, mat='STRAW'))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='carry', x=1.9, z=0.8, facing=0, sex='f', load='ROOT', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='squat_play', x=-1.8, z=0.9, facing=60, age='small', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='run', x=0.5, z=1.1, facing=180, age='child', sex='m', phase=phase))
    g = ground('GRASS', sc.cv.h, sc.cv.w, size_px, seed)
    # camas in bloom: blue-violet flecks on the grass (drawn in the grass material's own steps
    # would lose the hue; the prairie flower belongs to the plants students, so a few flecks only)
    return render_scene(sc, 'GRASS', hour, (40, 45), seed=seed, ground_idx=g, post=_camas_post(seed))


def _camas_post(seed):
    def post(idx, sc):
        rng = rngs(seed + 11)
        out = idx.copy()
        H, W = idx.shape
        n = int(H * W * 0.03)
        ys = rng.integers(0, H, n)
        xs = rng.integers(0, W, n)
        for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
            if sc.cv.idx[y, x] == 0 and idx[y, x] // 8 == M['GRASS']:
                out[y, x] = M['CAMAS'] * 8 + (3 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 2) if 'CAMAS' in M else idx[y, x]
        return out
    return post


def scene_canoe(size_px=24, elev=12, hour='golden', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """A canoe coming in to the beach at evening, four paddlers; people waiting on the gravel."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 6.5, 2.4, elev, 2.0)
    sc.add_prims(canoe(-0.6, -1.0, length=4.2, heading=0, waterline=0.0, beam=0.5))
    for i, x in enumerate((-2.0, -1.2, -0.4, 0.4)):
        sc.add_person(dict(activity='paddle', x=x, z=-1.0, facing=0, sex='m' if i % 2 == 0 else 'f',
                           phase=(phase + i * 0.08) % 1, seed=seed + i))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='stand', x=1.6, z=0.8, facing=160, sex='f', dress='coast_f_wool', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='hold_baby', x=2.2, z=0.95, facing=190, sex='f', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='throw', x=0.8, z=1.1, facing=150, age='child', sex='m', phase=phase))

    def post(idx, sc):
        out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -2.6, 'FOREST', seed)
        return np.where(sc.cv.idx > 0, idx, np.where(out != idx, out, idx))
    return render_scene(sc, 'GRAVEL', hour, (15, 20), seed=seed, water=dict(wl=0.0, z_shore=0.3, calm=0.75),
                        post=post)


def scene_play(size_px=24, elev=12, hour='noon', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """Children at play on the beach: running, throwing stones, squatting over shells; a mother
    watching."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 5.0, 2.0, elev, 1.65)
    kids = [(-1.6, 0.1, 0, 'run', 'child', 'm'), (-0.9, 0.4, 180, 'run', 'child', 'f'),
            (-0.1, -0.2, 30, 'throw', 'child', 'm'), (0.7, 0.3, 200, 'squat_play', 'child', 'f'),
            (1.2, 0.5, 250, 'squat_play', 'small', 'm')]
    for i, (x, z, f, a, age, sx) in enumerate(kids):
        sc.add_person(dict(activity=a, x=x, z=z, facing=f, age=age, sex=sx, phase=(phase + i * 0.23) % 1,
                           seed=seed + i))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='weave', x=1.9, z=-0.2, facing=190, sex='f', phase=phase))
    return render_scene(sc, 'SAND', hour, (40, 40), seed=seed)


def scene_work(size_px=24, elev=14, hour='noon', phase=0.0, seed=0):
    """Village work by the houses: a man splitting cedar, a woman weaving on a mat, another
    pounding dried salmon, fish on the rack, a woman walking by with a basket."""
    sc = _scene(size_px, 5.6, 2.3, elev, 1.85)
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='split', x=-1.8, z=0.0, facing=10, sex='m', phase=phase))
    sc.add_prims(mat_on_ground(-0.3, 0.2))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='weave', x=-0.3, z=0.2, facing=80, sex='f', phase=phase))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='pound', x=0.7, z=0.5, facing=160, sex='f', phase=phase))
    sc.add_prims(rack(1.6, -0.6, w=1.4, h=1.0, fish=7, seed=seed))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='carry', x=2.2, z=0.6, facing=180, sex='f', phase=phase, load='ROOT'))
    return render_scene(sc, 'DIRT', hour, (40, 40), seed=seed)


SCENES = dict(fire=scene_fire, weir=scene_weir, carry=scene_carry, camas=scene_camas, canoe=scene_canoe,
              play=scene_play, work=scene_work)

Bash: Better backdrops and weir layout
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('scenes.py').read()
old=s[s.index("def backdrop("):s.index("def _mix_idx")]
new='''def backdrop(idx, sc, z_far, mat='FOREST', crest_seed=0, amp=0.35, height=1.3):
    """Paint the rows beyond depth z_far as a far band: a forest wall whose top is a fringe of
    conifer spires (FOREST), or a flat far shore of water (WATER) with a lighter horizon line."""
    H, W = idx.shape
    rng = rngs(crest_seed)
    row = int(round(sc.oy + z_far * math.sin(sc.cv.e) * sc.scale))
    out = idx.copy()
    from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
    cl = gaussian_filter(rng.random((H, W)), 1.0)
    cl = (cl - cl.mean()) / (cl.std() + 1e-9)
    if mat == 'WATER':
        for yy in range(0, max(0, min(H, row))):
            for xx in range(W):
                st = 2 if cl[yy, xx] < 1.2 else 3
                if yy == row - 1:
                    st = 3
                out[yy, xx] = M['WATER'] * 8 + st
        return out, row
    heights = np.zeros(W)
    x = 0
    while x < W:
        w = int(rng.integers(max(2, sc.scale // 7), max(3, sc.scale // 4) + 1))
        hgt = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0) * sc.scale * 0.7
        for i in range(w):
            if x + i < W:
                t = abs(i + 0.5 - w / 2) / (w / 2)
                heights[x + i] = max(heights[x + i], hgt * (1 - t) ** 0.9)
        x += max(1, int(w * rng.uniform(0.5, 0.9)))
    for xx in range(W):
        y0 = int(row - sc.scale * height * 0.5 - heights[xx])
        for yy in range(max(0, y0), max(0, min(H, row))):
            st = 1 if cl[yy, xx] < 0.8 else 2
            if yy >= row - max(1, sc.scale // 10):
                st = 0                                  # the dark foot of the forest
            out[yy, xx] = M[mat] * 8 + st
    return out, row


'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
# weir layout
s=s.replace("""    sc = _scene(size_px, 7.0, 2.8, elev, 2.3)
    wz = -1.6""","""    sc = _scene(size_px, 7.0, 3.1, elev, 2.7)
    wz = -2.2""")
s=s.replace("out, _ = backdrop(idx, sc, -3.2, 'FOREST', seed)","out, _ = backdrop(idx, sc, -5.5, 'FOREST', seed)")
s=s.replace("out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -1.4, 'WATER', seed, amp=-0.9)","out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -2.0, 'WATER', seed)")
s=s.replace("out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -2.6, 'FOREST', seed)","out, row = backdrop(idx, sc, -5.0, 'FOREST', seed)")
open('scenes.py','w').write(s)
EOF

Write: elements/people/src/sheets.py
"""Labelled parameter sweeps.

A  activity x pose phase (24 px)
B  age and sex x dress (standing and walking, 24 px)
C  facing x size in px (walking woman, 32 -> 6 px)
D  light and hour x activity
E  seed x group (the same carrying file, individual variation from the seed)
F  groups at several distances (32, 16, 8, 5 px per person) at the landscape view
G  groups at the iso view (30 deg)
H  the smallest sizes at 1x and 2x (how they read at true scale)

Usage: python3 sheets.py [A B C ...]
"""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import os
import numpy as np
from render import Scene, render_scene
from poses import ACTIVITIES
from person import DRESS
from scenes import SCENES
from util import up, save, grid, side, vstack
OUT = '../sheets/'
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

SUN = dict(morning=(20, 22), noon=(40, 55), golden=(170, 14), overcast=(40, 50), dusk=(175, 6), night=(40, 40))


def person_cell(spec, size, kind='SAND', hour='noon', w=1.7, h=1.4, k=None, elev=10, sun=None):
    W, H = int(size * w), int(size * h)
    sc = Scene(W, H, size, elev, ground_y=H - max(2, size // 7))
    sc.add_person(spec)
    rgb, _ = render_scene(sc, kind, hour, sun or SUN[hour], seed=spec.get('seed', 0))
    return up(rgb, k or max(2, 144 // size))


def defaults(a):
    sex = 'm' if a in ('split', 'spear', 'dipnet', 'paddle', 'carry_load') else 'f'
    age = 'child' if a in ('run', 'squat_play', 'throw') else ('elder' if a == 'staff' else 'adult')
    return dict(activity=a, sex=sex, age=age)


def sheet_A():
    cells, labs = [], []
    for a in ACTIVITIES:
        for ph in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
            cells.append(person_cell(dict(defaults(a), phase=ph, facing=15, seed=3), 24, k=5))
            labs.append(f'{a} phase {ph}')
    save(grid(cells, 8, labs, fs=11, title='A. activity x pose phase, 24 px, noon, facing 15 deg'),
         OUT + 'A_activity_x_phase.png')


def sheet_B():
    cells, labs = [], []
    groups = [('adult f', 'f', 'adult'), ('adult m', 'm', 'adult'), ('elder f', 'f', 'elder'), ('elder m', 'm', 'elder'),
              ('child f', 'f', 'child'), ('child m', 'm', 'child'), ('small', 'm', 'small')]
    dresses = {'f': ['coast_f_summer', 'coast_f_rain', 'coast_f_wool', 'coast_f_hide', 'plateau_f', 'plateau_f_robe'],
               'm': ['coast_m_summer', 'coast_m_blanket', 'coast_m_fur', 'coast_m_hide', 'coast_m_rain', 'plateau_m']}
    for name, sex, age in groups:
        ds = dresses[sex] if age in ('adult', 'elder') else ['child_bare', 'child_robe', 'child_bare', 'child_robe',
                                                             'child_bare', 'child_robe']
        for i, d in enumerate(ds):
            reg = 'plateau' if d.startswith('plateau') else 'coast'
            act = 'walk' if i % 2 else 'stand'
            cells.append(person_cell(dict(activity=act, sex=sex, age=age, dress=d, region=reg, facing=30 + 40 * i,
                                          phase=0.1, seed=i + 7), 24, 'GRASS' if reg == 'plateau' else 'SAND',
                                     k=5))
            labs.append(f'{name}: {d}')
    save(grid(cells, 6, labs, fs=11, title='B. age and sex x dress (pre-horse, AD 1400-1730), 24 px'),
         OUT + 'B_age_sex_x_dress.png')


def sheet_C():
    cells, labs = [], []
    sizes = (32, 24, 16, 12, 9, 6)
    for f in (0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315):
        for s in sizes:
            cells.append(person_cell(dict(activity='walk', phase=0.1, facing=f, seed=5), s, k=int(6 * 32 / 32)))
            labs.append(f'facing {f}, {s} px')
    save(grid(cells, len(sizes), labs, fs=11, title='C. facing x size (walking woman), all at 6x'),
         OUT + 'C_facing_x_size.png')


def sheet_D():
    cells, labs = [], []
    acts = [('carry', 'f'), ('dig', 'f'), ('spear', 'm'), ('split', 'm'), ('fire', 'f')]
    for h in ('morning', 'noon', 'golden', 'overcast', 'dusk', 'night'):
        for a, sex in acts:
            if h == 'night':
                # at night only the fire lights people: render the fire-tending figure with a hearth
                from setting import hearth, paint_fire
                W, H = int(24 * 1.7), int(24 * 1.4)
                sc = Scene(W, H, 24, 12, ground_y=H - 4)
                sc.add_person(dict(activity=a, sex=sex, facing=15, phase=0.5, seed=2, x=-0.25))
                sc.add_prims(hearth(0.35, 0.1))
                fz = ((0.35, 0.12, 0.1), 0.5)

                def post(idx, sc):
                    p = sc.screen((0.35, 0, 0.1))
                    return paint_fire(idx, p[0], p[1] - 0.5, 24, 0.4, zbuf=sc.cv.z, zfire=p[2])
                rgb, _ = render_scene(sc, 'DIRT', 'night', (40, 40), fires=[fz], post=post)
                cells.append(up(rgb, 5))
            else:
                cells.append(person_cell(dict(activity=a, sex=sex, facing=15, phase=0.5, seed=2), 24,
                                         'GRASS' if a == 'dig' else 'SAND', h, k=5))
            labs.append(f'{h}: {a}')
    save(grid(cells, len(acts), labs, fs=11, title='D. light and hour x activity, 24 px'), OUT + 'D_hour_x_activity.png')


def sheet_E():
    rows = []
    for seed in range(4):
        rgb, _ = SCENES['carry'](20, seed=seed, phase=seed * 0.21)
        rows.append(side([up(rgb, 3)], [f'seed {seed}: stature, build, skin tone, hair and phase offsets vary']))
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 'E_seed_x_group.png')


def sheet_F():
    for name in SCENES:
        rows = []
        for s, k in ((32, 2), (16, 4), (8, 8), (5, 12)):
            rgb, _ = SCENES[name](s)
            rows.append(side([up(rgb, k)], [f'{name}: {s} px per person (x{k})']))
        save(vstack(rows), OUT + f'F_{name}_distances.png')


def sheet_G():
    rows = []
    for name in ('fire', 'carry', 'camas', 'work', 'play'):
        a, _ = SCENES[name](20, elev=30)
        b, _ = SCENES[name](10, elev=30)
        rows.append(side([up(a, 3), up(b, 6)], [f'{name}, iso 30 deg, 20 px', f'{name}, iso 30 deg, 10 px']))
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 'G_iso_30.png')


def sheet_H():
    rows = []
    for name in ('carry', 'weir', 'camas', 'canoe'):
        rgbs = [SCENES[name](s)[0] for s in (12, 8, 6, 5)]
        rows.append(side([up(r, 1) for r in rgbs] + [up(r, 2) for r in rgbs],
                         [f'{name} {s}px 1x' for s in (12, 8, 6, 5)] + [f'2x' for s in (12, 8, 6, 5)], fs=10))
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 'H_true_scale.png')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    which = sys.argv[1:] or list('ABCDEFGH')
    for w in which:
        globals()['sheet_' + w]()
        print('sheet', w, 'done')

    """Bruegel's stubble field. calm: his field is two close golds with short horizontal strokes of
    a third; the figures carry the only strong darks and lights nearby."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cols = np.array([[196, 160, 90], [182, 146, 78], [214, 184, 116], [150, 110, 50]], np.uint8)
    if not calm:
        return cols[rng.choice(4, size=(h, w), p=[0.45, 0.3, 0.15, 0.1])]
    a = np.where(rng.random((h, w)) < 0.35, 1, 0)
    for _ in range(int(h * w * 0.03)):
        y, x = rng.integers(0, h), rng.integers(0, w)
        a[y, x:x + rng.integers(2, 5)] = 2 if rng.random() < 0.6 else 3
    return cols[a]

        m = idx > 0
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m)
        under = np.zeros_like(m)
        for x in np.unique(xs):

            for d in (1, 2):

64. elements/people/sheets/G_iso_30.png

Write: elements/people/LESSONS.md
# People of pre-contact Washington, small and with dignity: lessons

A student's log. Subject: the people of Washington before horses and before smallpox (about AD
1400–1730, as the naturalist recommends), at work, at rest and at play, from about 30 px tall down to
6 px in a landscape, and from the 30° iso view.

**Accuracy** comes from the naturalist's human-ecology desk (`~/work/naturalist/people/`, guides 00, 03,
06 and its pruned photo folders). Their dress table (Haeberlin & Gunther 1930, Eells 1877, Spier &
Sapir 1930) is the spec for every garment here. **Care:** everyday lifeways only; no ceremonial or
sacred subjects, no specific designs (baskets and blankets get generic bands in their real material
colours), no real tribal names, no site locations. I deleted three folders my own image searches
brought in (masks and dance regalia, Curtis's whaler), because they were ceremonial.

**Code** (`src/`):

- `fig.py`: skeleton (two-segment spine, arm IK), capsule primitives, the rasterizer.
- `poses.py`: 21 activities as keyframed joint angles over a phase.
- `person.py`: body, hair, dress, props, per-seed individual variation.
- `render.py`: two-family shading, occlusion seams, firelight, the edge rule, scene render.
- `pal.py`: materials and hour palettes.
- `grounds.py`: calm grounds, contact and cast shadows.
- `water.py`: exact mirror reflections from the z-buffer.
- `setting.py`: hearth, fire, smoke, canoe, weir, rack, mats.
- `scenes.py`: seven group compositions.
- `sheets.py`: the labelled sweeps.
- One script per stage (`stage0.py` … `stage5.py`).

Everything renders an **index canvas** (material × step), so the hour is only a palette swap, as the
studio's synthesis recommends.

---

## Stage 0: what survives of a master's small figures

Ferrari painted no people in the Living Worlds scenes we have, and his LucasArts sprites aren't in the
studio. The closest master for small figures read by task is the moodboard's Bruegel. I took three
figures from *The Harvesters* (1565): the mower, the kneeling binder, and the woman bent double over
a sheaf. Each is 50–75 px tall in the 1280-px reproduction.

![reduction](stages/s0/s0_bruegel_reduction.png)

**Reducing them by area-averaging destroys them.** At ×0.5 (about 30 px) they still read. At ×0.25
(about 12 px) only the one lightest mass survives, the white shirt, as a blob. At ×0.125 they're gone,
indistinguishable from the stubble. What I take from it:

1. **A small figure is carried by one value extreme**: the lightest or darkest thing in its
   neighbourhood. For Bruegel it's the white shirt against the gold field. For our people it's black
   hair (always), a cream wool blanket, or a pale twined rain hat and cape. Bare skin and cedar bark
   sit in the ground's middle values. That's the danger case, and it is exactly the summer figure.
2. **Narrow things must be drawn, not averaged.** The scythe is a clean line at native size and noise
   after reduction. So the engine rasterizes at the target resolution, and every form under ~0.7 px
   radius is a clean Bresenham run: arms at 12 px, spears, stakes, paddles.
3. **The ground around a small figure must be calm.** Bruegel's stubble is two close golds with a few
   short strokes. The first version of my study field was salt-and-pepper, and it swallowed my
   figures. On the calm field (below) the same figures pop.
4. Bruegel always puts **a dark under the figure**: the shadow under the bent body, the dark feet.

![mine vs Bruegel](stages/s0/s0_bruegel_vs_mine.png)

My engine in his three poses and his colours (white shirts are the `WOOL` ramp, the red vest is
`BAND`), at the painting's size, half and quarter. **Honest comparison:** the poses now match in
gesture: the mower bent with the snath low, the woman bent double with a round back (this needed the
two-segment spine), the binder kneeling with hands in the sheaf. The masses don't match yet. His
figures are chunky, clothed volumes with a dark internal shadow tying them together. Mine are
slimmer, and my shirts are too white and too uniformly lit. At 11–13 px mine hold up about as well as
his reduced ones, because they're drawn at that size. That's the point, but his originals at 50 px
are simply better paintings.

---

## Stage 1: one figure, built up

![build-up](stages/s1/s1_buildup.png)

1. **Skeleton.** Pelvis, a two-segment spine (the upper back curls forward when bending: a round
   back, not a hinged plank), neck, head, arms with 2-bone IK for two-handed tools, legs posed
   against world vertical and grounded automatically: the lowest heel, toe, knee or buttock
   touches y = 0, or the pelvis sits on a seat. Bodies from Eells' measurements (Twana men averaged
   165 cm, 64 kg). Heads are ~1/7 of height at 30 px, growing to ~1/4.5 at 6 px, because small
   figures need their heads.
2. **Capsule masses.** Every part is a capsule (a radius profile along a segment), z-buffered per
   pixel at the target resolution. The torso is a chest bar, a waist in two halves (narrower in
   profile than from the front), a pelvis bar, buttocks behind and a small chest in front, so the
   profile has its S-curve. The hair is a sphere set back on the skull and the face a smaller
   capsule set forward, so from the front the hair frames the face and from the side it caps it.
   Loose hair falls down the back.
3. **Dress.** Garments are larger capsules that swallow the limbs inside them. A knee-length
   shredded-cedar-bark skirt: a flat-hemmed cone from the waist to the knees, with a darker
   waistband and vertical strands. A twined cape: a bell-profile cone from neck to hips. A blanket:
   a bell from the shoulders to the knee. Each gets a small depth bias so it wins over the legs even
   when its pixel floor inflates it.
4. **Two light families.** A pixel is lit (n·L > 0.04: halftone, light, or highlight above 22 px) or
   in shadow (shadow, or core on undersides above 12 px). Normals are blurred within each surface
   group first, so a torso made of five capsules shades as one form and not a patchwork. Far-side
   limbs sit one step down. Where a nearer part crosses a farther part, the farther one gets a
   one-step seam: an arm across the torso, a leg in front of a leg.
5. **Colour.** Skin, hair, bark and every other material is a five-step ramp walked in OKLab from a
   cool dark (the sky's hue) to a warm light (the sun's hue). Temperature moves only the chroma, never
   the lightness: my first ramps pulled the lightness toward the ambient colour and black hair came
   out slate blue.
6. **On the ground.** A contact line (the darkest step, under the feet), a cast shadow sheared along
   the light (the figure mask projected on the ground plane, solid near the feet and checker-dithered
   farther out, as Ferrari's soft shadows are), and the **edge rule**: a silhouette pixel whose value
   is within ~0.075 L of the ground beside it moves one step away from the ground's value. Found edges
   only where the figure would be lost.

![facings](stages/s1/s1_facings.png)

Notan, grey and colour from five sides. The notans hold in every view. **Not there yet:** at 32 px the
front view is still a little mannequin-like: shoulders square, hands just ends of lines. The 3/4-back
view (200°) shows almost no face, which is true but reads as "all hair".

---

## Stage 2: dress

![dress 32](stages/s2/s2_dress_32.png)

Fifteen outfits from guide 06's table, pre-horse:

| outfit | what it is |
|---|---|
| `coast_f_summer` | the knee-length bark skirt, bare above |
| `coast_f_rain` | skirt, twined cape and wide-brimmed hat: the cone-on-cone figure the guide calls the most distinctive on the coast, and it reads at every size |
| `coast_f_hide` | buckskin shirt to below the knee (the well-to-do) |
| `coast_f_wool` | the skirt with a cream blanket over the shoulders |
| `coast_m_summer` | breechclout |
| `coast_m_blanket` | breechclout and a cream blanket with an alder-red and a black band |
| `coast_m_fur` | fur robe |
| `coast_m_hide` | fringed shirt and leggings |
| `coast_m_rain` | cape and hat |
| `plateau_f`, `plateau_f_robe` | the apron-skirt and the twined basket hat, with or without a robe |
| `plateau_m`, `plateau_m_robe` | breechclout, hair in two queues in front, with or without a robe |
| `child_bare`, `child_robe` | children naked in summer, or in a little robe |

![dress 16](stages/s2/s2_dress_16.png)

**What reads at 16 px:** cream wool (a white rectangle), the rain hat (a pale disk), the grey fur robe,
and black hair. Bark and skin separate only by a step of value, and that's why the bark ramp is
darker and redder than the naturalist's suggested #8A5A3C. **Not there yet:** the buckskin shirt and
leggings read as pale pyjamas. The fringe under the arms is not drawn, and smoked-hide leggings would
help. The Plateau queues are invisible below 24 px.

---

## Stage 3: activities and their phases

![A](sheets/A_activity_x_phase.png)

21 activities, each keyframed over a phase:

- **walking and carrying:** walk, carry (tumpline basket), carry_load (salmon in each hand), staff (an elder);
- **tool work:** dig (digging stick), gather, split (hand maul and antler wedge on a cedar log), spear, dipnet, paddle, pound (stone pestle);
- **seated and resting:** weave, fire (tending, crouched), sit, sit_log, sit_eat, hold_baby (cradleboard);
- **play and idle:** run, squat_play, throw, stand.

What made them read:

- **The prop line is the activity.** A digging stick into the ground, a 15–25 ft salmon spear (the
  guide's length; held at its middle it rises far behind the fisherman, which looked wrong to me
  until I checked), a dip-net pole to the water, a paddle upright at the side. The first versions
  pointed the prop along the vector between the two hands, and it spun wildly between phases.
  Arm IK fixed it: the pose puts the two hands on the tool's line, and the prop is drawn through
  them.
- **Bend from the hips with a round back** for digging, gathering and binding. Millet's gleaners and
  Bruegel's binders both have it. A hinged plank reads as a bow.
- **Weight:** carrying leans forward from the ankles with shortened steps. The salmon carrier's
  arms hang straight with the weight. The splitter bends over the log, maul raised to the head,
  other hand on the wedge. My first splitter swung overhead with straight arms and read as waving.
- The walk is Muybridge's four keys (contact, down, passing, up), mirrored, with a small bob.

**Not there yet:** the throw and squat_play poses are weak at 24 px. The seated poses (sit, sit_eat)
merge into a blob of skirt and thigh. The paddler's blade is often hidden behind the body.

---

## Stage 4: the size ladder, 32 → 6 px

![ladder](stages/s4/s4_ladder_6x.png)
![ladder 2x](stages/s4/s4_ladder_2x.png)

**Rules for small sizes** (all in code, driven by `size_px`):

- **Head grows** from 1/7 to about 1/4.5 of height between 28 and 6 px.
- **Limbs and trunk thicken** by up to 45% below 12 px.
- **Pixel floors per mass:** a 6 px person is two pixels wide. The torso and skirt get floors of
  ~0.9 px radius at 6 px, falling to 0.5 at 10 px.
- **Below 10 px:** the face shrinks and the hair sphere rides up, so the head pixel is dark: a dark
  dot on top reads as "person" instantly. Occlusion seams switch off. Only the legs keep the
  far-side step, because it's what separates them.
- **Undersides go to the core step only above 12 px.** Below that, the dark foot pixel looked like a
  hole.
- I tried rendering at 4× and reducing by salience-weighted mode. It's cleaner in silhouette, but it
  lost the head in front views and blurred the skirt contrast. Direct rendering with floors won. The
  code is kept (`reduce_idx`, `sprite_ss`) for comparison.

At 2× (close to true size) the figures stay distinct people doing distinct things down to 11 px. At
8–6 px, pale garments carry them (the blanketed man is a white dot on legs, the naturalist's "pale
for blanketed figures"), and the rain hat and the tumpline basket keep their silhouettes. **Not there
yet:** bare summer figures on sand at 6–8 px depend entirely on the edge rule and the contact
shadow. They read, but weakly.

---

## Stage 5: light and hour

![hours](stages/s5/s5_hours_carry.png)

Same index canvas, six palettes. Each hour sets a sun colour, an ambient colour, and how hard the
lights are pulled warm and the shadows cool: `warm` / `cool`, from overcast 0.04 / 0.12 to golden
0.55 / 0.5. Water takes the sky's colour (sky top in its darks, horizon in its lights). Overcast uses
a top light and casts no shadows. Golden hour and dusk back-light the file: orange rims, violet
shadows and long checkered cast shadows. My first hour palettes differed so little that the sheet
looked like one image five times.

![fire](stages/s5/s5_fire_dusk_night.png)

**Night is firelight.** The fire is a point light low in the hearth. Every pixel gets a world position
from its screen position and z-buffer depth. Faces turned to the fire take the lit family, whose
steps in the night palette are warm orange. Everything else stays in a cool near-black ambient. The
ground is lifted in a dithered pool around the hearth (Bayer 2×2 at the falloff edge). Figures
between us and the fire become silhouettes, as in de La Tour and Biard. The flames are teardrop
tongues (a hot core in a cooler rim) whose tips flicker with phase, drawn behind any figure nearer
than the hearth.

---

## Stage 6: groups at several distances

Seven compositions in `scenes.py`, each rendered at any px-per-person and elevation:

| scene | what's in it |
|---|---|
| **fire** | the hearth at night: people sitting, tending, weaving, eating, a baby, a child |
| **weir** | dip-netters on platforms, a spear fisher in the shallows, a canoe, women carrying salmon to the rack |
| **carry** | a file along the beach: tumpline baskets, salmon, a rain cape, a blanket, an elder with a staff, children running ahead |
| **camas** | women digging on the May prairie, baskets beside them, children |
| **canoe** | four paddlers coming in at golden hour |
| **play** | children on the beach |
| **work** | splitting cedar, weaving on a mat, pounding, a drying rack |

![carry](sheets/F_carry_distances.png)
![weir](sheets/F_weir_distances.png)
![fire](sheets/F_fire_distances.png)
![camas](sheets/F_camas_distances.png)
![canoe](sheets/F_canoe_distances.png)
![iso](sheets/G_iso_30.png)

- **Props need their own level of detail.** At 8 px per person the weir's 30 stakes became a
  fence as loud as the people. Now stakes are never closer than ~3 px on screen, are dark wet
  poles, and lose their rails below 10–20 px.
- **Water is an exact mirror.** Every object pixel has a world position from the z-buffer, so it's
  reflected about the water plane and re-projected. Lights drop a step more than darks, dash rows
  break it, and anything below the plane is hidden (the fisher's shins, the paddlers inside the
  hull).
- Backdrops are plain on purpose: a forest wall with a crest of spires, or a far shore of water with
  sparse glints, and a banded sky. They belong to the other element students.

---

## Sheets

- `sheets/A_activity_x_phase.png`: activity × phase.
- `B_age_sex_x_dress.png`: age and sex × dress.
- `C_facing_x_size.png`: 8 facings × 32→6 px.
- `D_hour_x_activity.png`: hour × activity, including night by firelight.
- `E_seed_x_group.png`: individual variation from the seed (stature ±4%, build, three skin tones,
  hair loose or tied, phase offsets).
- `F_*_distances.png`: each group at 32, 16, 8 and 5 px per person.
- `G_iso_30.png`: groups at the 30° iso view.
- `H_true_scale.png`: the small sizes at 1× and 2×.

Entry point: `person.build(spec, size_px)` → prims; `render.Scene` + `render.render_scene` for a
scene; `scenes.SCENES[name](size_px, elev, hour, phase, seed, sun)`.

Bash: Walk vs Muybridge comparison
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && mkdir -p ../stages/s3 && cat > stage3.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 3: activities. The walk against Muybridge; the sheet A of all activities is in sheets/."""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np, os
from PIL import Image
from render import Scene, render_scene, shade, light_vec
from pal import palette
from util import up, save, side, vstack
OUT = '../stages/s3/'; os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
MUY = '../refs/Muybridge_woman_walking/commons_A_naked_woman_walking_Collotype_after_Muybridge__be0c3a0f.jpg'


def muybridge_frames(size=32):
    im = Image.open(MUY).convert('L')
    x0, y0, cw, ch = 260, 305, 124.7, 136
    out = []
    for i in range(6):
        c = im.crop((int(x0 + i * cw), y0, int(x0 + (i + 1) * cw) - 4, y0 + ch))
        a = np.asarray(c).astype(float)
        # the figure is light on a darker gridded ground: find its bounding box by threshold
        m = a > np.percentile(a, 88)
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m[5:-18])
        ys = ys + 5
        top, bot = ys.min(), ys.max()
        k = size / (bot - top)
        small = np.asarray(c.resize((max(1, int(c.width * k)), max(1, int(c.height * k))), Image.BOX))
        out.append((small, k, top, bot))
    return out


def mine(phase, size=32, facing=180):
    sc = Scene(int(size * 1.2), int(size * 1.25), size, 8, ground_y=int(size * 1.15))
    sc.add_person(dict(activity='walk', phase=phase, facing=facing, dress='child_bare', hair='tied', seed=1))
    rgb, idx = render_scene(sc, 'SAND', 'noon', (40, 40))
    m = sc.cv.idx > 0
    sil = np.where(m[..., None], np.uint8(30), np.uint8(200)).repeat(3, -1)
    return rgb, sil


if __name__ == '__main__':
    fr = muybridge_frames()
    row1 = [up(np.stack([f[0]] * 3, -1), 6) for f in fr]
    row2 = []
    for f in fr:
        a = f[0].astype(float)
        m = a > np.percentile(a, 80)
        row2.append(up(np.where(m[..., None], 30, 200).astype(np.uint8).repeat(3, -1), 6))
    phases = [0.0, 1 / 6, 2 / 6, 3 / 6, 4 / 6, 5 / 6]
    mm = [mine(p) for p in phases]
    rows = [side(row1, ['Muybridge (area-reduced to 32 px)'] + [''] * 5),
            side(row2, ['his silhouette (threshold)'] + [''] * 5),
            side([up(s, 6) for _, s in mm], ['my walk, silhouette'] + [''] * 5),
            side([up(r, 6) for r, _ in mm], ['my walk, 32 px'] + [''] * 5)]
    save(vstack(rows), OUT + 's3_walk_vs_muybridge.png')
EOF
python3 stage3.py

Bash: IK to joints; seated poses
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('fig.py').read()
a=s.index("    # shoulders with twist about the trunk axis")
b=s.index("    # legs relative to world vertical")
c=s.index("    # grounding")
arms=s[a:b]; legs=s[b:c]
s=s[:a]+legs+arms+s[c:]
s=s.replace("""            T = chest + v3(*ik) * P.get('stature', 1.0)""","""            if isinstance(ik, str):          # reach for a joint: 'knL', 'knR', 'anL' ...
                T = J[ik] + v3(0.0, 0.02, 0.0)
            elif len(ik) == 4:               # (joint, df, dy, dl): an offset from a joint
                T = J[ik[0]] + v3(*ik[1:])
            else:

# output:
"""Load Ferrari's Living Worlds scenes as index maps (format from the Canvas Cycle demo)."""
import re, json, numpy as np
REF = "~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/master-copy/ref/lw"
def load(name):
    t = open(f"{REF}/{name}.json").read()
    t = re.sub(r'([{,])\s*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*:', r'\1"\2":', t).replace("'", '"')
    d = json.loads(t)
    d["pal"] = np.array(d["colors"], dtype=np.uint8)
    d["idx"] = np.array(d["pixels"], dtype=np.int32).reshape(d["height"], d["width"])
    return d
def lum(rgb):
    rgb = np.asarray(rgb, float)
    return 0.2126 * rgb[..., 0] + 0.7152 * rgb[..., 1] + 0.0722 * rgb[..., 2]

"""Load Ferrari's Living Worlds scenes as index maps (the willow student's loader, copied)."""
import re, json, numpy as np

def load(name):
    t = open(f"{REF}/{name}.json").read()
    t = re.sub(r'([{,])\s*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*:', r'\1"\2":', t).replace("'", '"')
    d = json.loads(t)
    d["pal"] = np.array(d["colors"], dtype=np.uint8)
    d["idx"] = np.array(d["pixels"], dtype=np.int32).reshape(d["height"], d["width"])
    return d

Bash: Measure Ferrari smoke and flame
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np
from lw import load
d=load('V01'); idx=d['idx'][0:110,300:420]; pal=d['pal']
smoke=(idx>=200)&(idx<=215)
sky=(idx>=1)&(idx<=9)
flame=np.isin(idx,[43,44,45,46,47]+list(range(100,120)))
print('smoke px',smoke.sum(),'flame px',flame.sum())
# in the bounding region of smoke (dilated), what fraction is smoke
from scipy.ndimage import binary_dilation, binary_closing
reg=binary_closing(smoke,iterations=2)
print('region px',reg.sum(),'smoke share in region',smoke[reg].mean())
# checker test: of smoke pixels in region, fraction whose 4-neighbours are all sky
nb=np.zeros_like(smoke)
for dy,dx in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):
    nb |= np.roll(np.roll(smoke,dy,0),dx,1)
iso=smoke & ~nb
print('isolated smoke pixels (checker-dithered)', iso.sum()/smoke.sum())
# smoke value by vertical position within plume
L=pal[idx].astype(float)@[0.2126,0.7152,0.0722]
ys,xs=np.nonzero(smoke)
for y0 in range(0,110,20):
    s=(ys>=y0)&(ys<y0+20)
    if s.sum(): print('rows',y0,'mean L',L[ys[s],xs[s]].mean().round(1),'n',s.sum())
# light on top vs bottom edges
top=smoke & ~np.roll(smoke,1,0)
bot=smoke & ~np.roll(smoke,-1,0)
print('top-edge L',L[top].mean().round(1),'bottom-edge L',L[bot].mean().round(1),'interior',L[smoke&~top&~bot].mean().round(1))
print('flame colours', [tuple(pal[i]) for i in np.unique(idx[flame])])
EOF

Bash: New smoke painter and Ferrari fire copy
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('setting.py').read()
a=s.index("def paint_smoke(")
s=s[:a]+'''def paint_smoke(idx, cx, cy, size_px, phase=0.0, seed=0, drift=0.6, rows=None, hour_dark=False, wind=1.0):
    """Woodsmoke after Ferrari (V01's torch plume, measured): a coherent billowing plume whose
    value sits close to the background, hue-shifted; a solid core, checker-dithered margins
    (~40% of his smoke pixels are isolated checker pixels), sparse dither at the thin ends; the
    edges a touch lighter than the interior. It rises, then leans downwind and widens."""
    from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    H, W = idx.shape
    out = idx.copy()
    L = int(rows or size_px * 1.8)
    ys, xs = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]
    t = np.clip((cy - (ys + 0.5)) / max(L, 1), 0, 1.5)             # 0 at the source, 1 at the top
    # centreline: rises, then bends downwind
    xc = cx + wind * drift * L * (t ** 1.6) * 0.9 + np.sin(t * 5 + phase * 6.28) * size_px * 0.06 * t
    half = size_px * (0.04 + 0.28 * t ** 0.9)
    env = np.clip(1 - np.abs(xs + 0.5 - xc) / np.maximum(half, 0.5), 0, 1) * (t > 0) * np.clip(1.25 - t, 0, 1)
    # billows: smooth noise advected upward with the phase
    n1 = gaussian_filter(rng.random((H + L, W)), max(1.0, size_px * 0.08))
    off = int(phase * L) % L
    n1 = n1[off:off + H]
    n1 = (n1 - n1.mean()) / (n1.std() + 1e-9)
    D = env * (0.75 + 0.35 * n1) * (1 - 0.35 * t)
    chk = (xs + ys) % 2 == 0
    sparse = (xs % 2 == 0) & (ys % 2 == 0)
    solid = D > 0.62
    mid = (D > 0.36) & ~solid & chk
    thin = (D > 0.2) & ~solid & (D <= 0.36) & sparse
    m = solid | mid | thin
    # the edges of the solid core are a touch lighter (lit), the core darker at the base
    edge = solid & ~(np.roll(solid, 1, 0) & np.roll(solid, -1, 0) & np.roll(solid, 1, 1) & np.roll(solid, -1, 1))
    st = np.where(edge, 2, 1)
    st = np.where(~solid, 2, st)
    if not hour_dark:
        st = st + 1
    st = np.where(t < 0.12, np.minimum(st, 1 + (not hour_dark)), st)
    out[m] = M['SMOKE'] * 8 + st[m]
    return out
'''
open('setting.py','w').write(s)
EOF
cat > stage0_fire.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 0b: a master copy of Ferrari's fire and smoke (Living Worlds V01, the torch on the
peak), because hearths are where people gather. His pixels | my painters on his sky."""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')
import numpy as np, os
from lw import load
from setting import paint_fire, paint_smoke
from pal import M
from util import up, save, side
OUT = '../stages/s0/'

if __name__ == '__main__':
    d = load('V01')
    idx = d['idx'][0:110, 300:420]
    pal = d['pal']
    his = pal[idx]
    smoke = (idx >= 200) & (idx <= 215)
    flame = np.isin(idx, [43, 44, 45, 46, 47] + list(range(100, 120)))
    # his sky without the plume: each smoke/flame pixel takes the sky index most common in its row
    sky = idx.copy()
    for y in range(idx.shape[0]):
        row = idx[y][(idx[y] >= 1) & (idx[y] <= 9)]
        if len(row):
            v = np.bincount(row).argmax()
            sky[y][smoke[y] | flame[y]] = v
    base = pal[sky]
    # my painters in index space, mapped to his colours: smoke steps -> his smoke ramp, fire -> his
    canvas = np.zeros(idx.shape, np.int16)
    fx, fy = 44, 62                                      # the brazier's top, in the crop
    c2 = paint_smoke(canvas, fx + 3, fy - 5, 30, phase=0.2, seed=3, drift=1.2, rows=60, hour_dark=True,
                     wind=1.0)
    c2 = paint_fire(c2, fx, fy, 22, phase=0.35)
    sm_cols = {1: pal[205], 2: pal[211], 3: pal[212]}
    fi_cols = {1: pal[45], 2: pal[47], 3: pal[114], 4: pal[116] if 116 < len(pal) else pal[114]}
    mine = base.copy()
    for s, c in sm_cols.items():
        mine[c2 == M['SMOKE'] * 8 + s] = c
    for s, c in fi_cols.items():
        mine[c2 == M['FIRE'] * 8 + s] = c
    save(side([up(his, 6), up(mine, 6)], ['Ferrari V01 (native)', 'my fire and smoke painters, his palette']),
         OUT + 's0_fire_copy.png')
EOF

Bash: Two-octave smoke lobes
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/people/src && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('setting.py').read()
old=s[s.index("    # billows: smooth noise advected upward with the phase"):s.index("    out[m] = M['SMOKE'] * 8 + st[m]")]
new="""    # billows: two octaves of smooth noise advected upward with the phase (lobes, curls, holes)
    off = int(phase * L) % max(L, 1)
    def octave(sig, s_):
        n = gaussian_filter(np.random.default_rng(seed + s_).random((H + L, W)), sig)[off:off + H]
        return (n - n.mean()) / (n.std() + 1e-9)
    n1 = 0.7 * octave(max(1.0, L * 0.08), 1) + 0.45 * octave(max(0.8, L * 0.035), 2)
    D = env ** 0.6 * (0.74 + 0.42 * n1 * np.minimum(1, t * 2.5)) * (1 - 0.25 * t)
    chk = (xs + ys) % 2 == 0
    sparse = (xs % 2 == 0) & (ys % 2 == 0)
    solid = D > 0.7
    mid = (D > 0.42) & ~solid & chk
    thin = (D > 0.26) & (D <= 0.42) & sparse
    m = solid | mid | thin
    # inside the solid lobes: tone by density (denser lobes catch more light), lit upper edges
    top = solid & ~np.roll(solid, 1, 0)
    st = np.where(D > 0.9, 2, 1)
    st = np.where(top, 2, st)
    st = np.where(~solid, 1, st)
    if not hour_dark:
        st = st + 1
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open('setting.py','w').write(s)
EOF
